Bara uru maka buut ngwa ngwa na nnukwu ọdụ data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(Edobeghi)_                         | systemd (sd_notify protocol)                                             | Tọọ site na sistemụ mgbe usoro a na-aga n'okpuru ngalaba ọrụ yana ntinye sd_notify; OmniRoute gụrụ ya (lee `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) iji zipu ọkwa READY/WATCHDOG. Onye ọrụ edobeghi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Tọọ `1` iji gbanyụọ systemd sd_notify (Ụdị = notify / WatchdogSec=) ọbụlagodi mgbe ị na-agba ọsọ n'okpuru ngalaba sistemụ. Ihe ngosi bụ enweghị op n'èzí sistemu n'agbanyeghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                            | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Ogologo oge (ms) maka onye nhazi nyocha ahụike ndabere ndabere. Opekempe: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                             | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) maka ọkwa ahụike echekwabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                              | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Tọọ `1` ma ọ bụ `true` iji gbanyụọ nyocha ndabere oge nke njikọ ndị na-eweta. Ndị na-achọ ihe (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS na `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, dịka `tavily-search`) na-ewepu mgbe niile na — "nkwalite" ha bụ ajụjụ a na-akwụ ụgwọ n'ezie, n'ihi ya, a naghị enyocha ahụike na ngụ oge (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                  | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Tọọ na `1` ka ikwe ka onye na-akpọ oku kwenye ka ọ rịọ `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, nke na-egosi n'elu mmecha ya otu ugboro kwa TTL. Ndị na-akpọ oku amaghị aha anaghị akpalite nyocha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                               | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Ọnụ ụzọ nkwụsị (ms) maka ihe ndekọ ihe omume-loop boot: friza ogologo oge karịa nke a na-edekọ otu ahịrị `event loop stalled` na malitegharịa yana ebe nchekwa na ọnụ ọgụgụ ụgbọ elu, wee kụda `eventLoopStall` counter/maximum na `/api/monitoring/health`. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ; uru enweghị ike ịgụ ma ọ bụ nke na-adịghị mma na-adaghachi na `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Kechie adreesị maka sava Next.js dev/start. Na-ewepụ `0.0.0.0` ndabara mgbe atọrọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adreesị ejikọta nke onye na-agba ọsọ Playwright na-eji mgbe ọ na-ebupute Next.js. Ihe ndabara na `127.0.0.1` maka ule hermetic.** Ejila `omniroute serve`** — jiri `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` kama (POSIX shells auto-set `HOSTNAME` na aha igwe; `.env` enweghị ike ịkagbu ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                            | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Kechie adreesị maka `omniroute serve`. Na-ezere nkukota na POSIX shei `HOSTNAME` variable (na-edobe aha igwe mgbe niile site na bash/zsh). Daghachi azụ na `0.0.0.0` mgbe ịtọghe. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### Ụdị ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri

```
┌─────────────────────────── Single Port (default) ──────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Split Ports ─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                     │
│  Use case: Expose API to LAN while restricting Dashboard to localhost.      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Docker Production ──────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Maps container ports to host ports in docker-compose.prod.yml.          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Nchekwa & Nyocha

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                                                                                           | Faịlụ Isi                                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                                             | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Salt a na-ejikọta na ihe nchọpụta ngwaike maka ịchọpụta akara mkpịsị aka igwe. Gbanwee ya maka deployment ọ bụla iji kewapụ ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(anaghị edobe = salt na-enweghị usoro maka nrụnye ọ bụla, nke a na-echekwa na `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | HMAC salt maka iwepụta akara nkwenye CLI nke mpaghara. Ịtọ uru a na-atụgharị akara CLI niile dị na igwe ahụ, ọ na-ebukwa ụzọ mgbe niile karịa salt echekwara. Hụ `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                                           | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Na-ahazi ọkọlọtọ `Secure` na kuki nnọkọ. **Ọ GHAGHỊ ịbụ `true`** mgbe a na-agba ya n'azụ HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                                           | API middleware                                           | Mgbe enyere ya aka, onye ahịa API proxy chọrọ nzere anabatara maka `/api/v1/*` na `/v1/*`, gụnyere `GET /v1/models` n'ihu onye na-elekọta ya. Asambodo na-agbaso amụma API onye ahịa ( igodo API ma ọ bụ nnọkọ anabatara anabatara). Katalọgụ ahụ nwekwara nlele `requireAuthForModels` onye na-ahụ maka ya, nke enweghị ike ịgafe proxy. Na ọkọlọtọ zuru ụwa ọnụ kwụsịrị, katalọgụ ka nwere ike ịlaghachi `401`; na nzaghachi naanị adịghị egosi na a na-echebe inference. Lee `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695). Nabata `true`, `1` ma ọ bụ `yes`, na-eleghara ikpe na oghere ndị gbara ya gburugburu; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere `on`) agbanyụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                                           | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Na-enye ohere igosi ụkpụrụ igodo API zuru ezu na UI Dashboard. Enwere ike ịhazi ya site na Ọkọlọtọ Njirimara Dashboard; ọ bụ ihe ize ndụ nchekwa n'ọnọdụ ndị mmadụ na-ekekọrịta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(efu)_                                                                                                           | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | NJirimara igodo API ndị a kewara site na rikoma, nke na-agafe ndekọ arịrịọ (nrube isi GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(edoghị etinye = enweghị oke)_                                                                                   | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Mmefu arịrịọ kwa ụbọchị ndabere a na-etinye n’ọrụ na igodo API ndị kọlụm `rate_limits` ha bụ null. Mgbe edoghị ya ma ọ bụ ọ tọgbọ chakoo: enweghị oke zoro ezo (#2289, #11017). `0` bụ otu ihe ahụ (enweghị oke). Ọnụọgụ zuru ezu N dị mma na-eme ka N kwa ụbọchị, 5N kwa izu, na 20N kwa ọnwa rụọ ọrụ. Uru ndị na-abụghị efu ma hazighị nke ọma na-alaghachi na windo ochie nke 1000 kwa ụbọchị, 5000 kwa izu, na 20000 kwa ọnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Otu ụzọ mgbapụ [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) maka ohere isi dị mma ka nke structure-heavy dị; mana `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) ka na-egbochi ya ka [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ghara ịlaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(unset — no request-count cap)_                                                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** Oke ọnụọgụ arịrịọ ochie a na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe e setịpụrụ ya n'ụzọ doro anya. Mgbe a hapụrụ ya ka ọ ghara ịtọ (nke bụ ndabara), `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` na-achịkwa nnabata nkata buru ibu kama — ọ bụ mmefu ego BYTE a na-emepụta na-akpaghị aka, nke a tụrụ site n'ókè ncheta nke usoro ahụ n'ezie n'ime **otu usoro** (otu ikpo V8). Arịrịọ `/v1/responses` abụọ na-agbakọta, nke nke ọ bụla nwere ihe dị ka token 750k, na-eme ka ikpo ~12 Gi kwụsị (#7849) — nke a bụ ịdọ aka ná ntị gbasara **mmefu ego ncheta**, ọ bụghị oke ngwaahịa siri ike nke 2. Usoro dị mma (ikpo ya dị n'okpuru oke ịbelata) NWERE IKE ịnabata ọtụtụ `/v1/responses` ogologo na-agba n'otu oge site na `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Ọtụtụ ndị ahịa SSE ogologo (40–50) metụtara ikpo + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ọ bụghị “max 2”. Ịkwalite nke a n'enweghị nyocha iji “jiri host ahụ” na-eweghachite #7849. Jikọta **ikpo** site na **N `DATA_DIR`s nọọrọ onwe ha** (#11024); etinyela `replicas>1` n'otu faịlụ SQLite.                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(auto-derived)_                                                                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** Nhọrọ iji dochie mmefu ego byte nke nnabata a na-emepụta na-akpaghị aka (25% nke ókè ncheta V8/cgroup kacha nta, kewara site na mmụba nwa oge nke 8x). Uru ndị a na-emepụta na nke e depụtara n'ụzọ doro anya na-adaba n'ókè 8 MiB–2 GiB. Ahụ nke karịrị mmefu ego dị irè na-ada ozugbo na `413 body_exceeds_budget`; esemokwu dị n'etiti ahụ ndị nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu n'otu ka na-enwe ike ịnwale ọzọ site na `503`. Ndị ahịa SSE ogologo 40–50 na-agba n'otu oge metụtara mmefu ego a + ikpo, ọ bụghị “max 2” siri ike. Gụọ `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` na `/api/monitoring/health` tupu ịhazigharịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Ọ na-enyekwa ohere maka private webhook targets, ewezuga cloud-metadata/link-local endpoints, nke ka na-anọgide na-egbochi ọbụlagodi mgbe ọkọlọtọ a gbanyere. Arịrịọ chat ndị a na-ezipụ site na `BaseExecutor.execute()` na-edobe nyocha cloud-metadata ha. Ndị provider mpaghara na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (ndabara `true`) enyela ohere maka URL mpaghara na LAN na validation, discovery, na provider-node paths; ule proxy-fallback na private webhook targets na-ele naanị ọkọlọtọ a anya ma na-egbochi host mpaghara/LAN mgbe ọ gbanyụrụ. Ọ na-emeri `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Uru e chekwara site na dashboard toggle na-ebute ụzọ karịa variable a. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                                            | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Nye ohere maka provider URLs dị na adreesị mpaghara/onwe (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, private ranges) maka provider validation, model discovery, chat, na ụzọ outbound ndị ọzọ dị nso na provider nke ọnọdụ guard na-achịkwa. **Default `true`** (local-first): guard ahụ na-egbochi cloud-metadata endpoints — 169.254.0.0/16 niile tinyere aha host metadata ndị a ma ama (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Tọọ `false` maka ọnọdụ public-only, nke na-egbochikwa host private na loopback. Guard ahụ na-enyocha hostname ma ọ bụ IP literal dịka e dere ya, tupu DNS lookup ọ bụla; n’ọnọdụ public-only, safe-fetch wrapper na-emezi hostname ahụ ọzọ ma jụ azịza private ọ bụla. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` na-agbanyụ nyocha ndị a n’ụzọ ndị ahịrị ya depụtara. Ndị provider mpaghara e wuru n’ime usoro ahụ, dịka LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, na Triton, na-awụpụ nyocha host niile maka key validation na chat. Uru e chekwara site na dashboard toggle na-ebute ụzọ karịa variable a. (#5066)             |

Ha ka na-eziga igodo API nke njikọ ahụ. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                                           | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Aha a na-akpọ `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` ọzọ (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_MFA`                     | `false`                                                                                                           | `src/server/auth/dashboardMfa.ts`                        | Nweghachi nnweta mgbe ngwaọrụ furu efu maka nbanye dashboard TOTP nwere nzọụkwụ nkwenye abụọ nke onye ọrụ họọrọ. Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, a na-awụpụ naanị ihe nkwenye nke abụọ mgbe nbanye sitere n’aka host ahụ n’onwe ya (loopback peer na loopback `Host`); nbanye sitere n’ebe dị anya na `POST /api/cli/connect` sitere na origins ndị ọzọ ka chọrọ koodu ahụ. Banye n’ime sistemụ n’obodo, gbanyụọ nkwenye nzọụkwụ abụọ n’okpuru Settings → Security (enwere ike ịhapụ koodu ahụ oghere mgbe nke a dị otu a, ka e wee wepụ ihe nkwenye na-adịghịzi akwado; a ka chọrọ paswọọdụ), wee wepụ ya. TOTP na-ekpuchi naanị nbanye paswọọdụ na `omniroute connect` — nbanye OIDC/SSO na-adabere n’ihe nkwenye nke abụọ nke onye na-enye njirimara n’onwe ya. Lee [AUTHZ_GUIDE](../architecture/AUTHZ_GUIDE.md#optional-totp-two-factor-login-16619).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Ndepụta Nyocha Maka Ịkwalite Nchekwa

```bash
# Opekempe nchekwa maka mmepụta:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Ọ chọrọ HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Nyochaa oku proxy niile
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Egosila igodo n’ime UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Oke 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Ntinye ịcha ọcha & PII Nchekwa

OmniRoute na-enye nchekwa nke oyi akwa abụọ: nyocha-n'akụkụ ịgbanye arịrịọ na iwepụ PII nzaghachi.

> **⚠️ :** Ihe nchekwa ndị a bụ nchọpụta _kachasị mbọ ike heuristic_, ọ bụghị ngwa ngwa ịgbanye ọkụ ọkụ ma ọ bụ sistemu PII DLP. Ha nwere ike iwepụta ihe nrịbama ụgha (benign persona/RPG kpaliri ọkọlọtọ) yana ihe ọjọọ (leetspeak, spacing, na-abụghị nke Bekee). Ha ezughị naanị maka nnabata. Dezie ụdịdị wee nwalee megide okporo ụzọ gị tupu ị dabere na ha.

### Akụkụ arịrịọ: Nchekwa ịgbanye ngwa ngwa

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kwado nyocha nke ozi mbata maka usoro ịgba ọgwụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                 |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Amụma ịgba ọgwụ: `warn` = log naanị, `block` = jụrụ arịrịọ ya na 400. Legacy `redact` na-eme ya.** ọ bụghị** ederede ịgba ntụtụ; jiri `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` maka arịrịọ PII degharịa. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Legacy utu aha maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                                             |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`       | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Ogo kacha nta nke `MODE=block` jụrụ: `high` (ndabere), `medium`, ma ọ bụ `low`. A na-ahụ ụkpụrụ nke ọkara ma ọ bụrụ na ewedara ya.                                                      |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Legacy utu aha maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                                  |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Mgbe `true`, megharịa PII na arịrịọ mbata (na-adabereghị na ọnọdụ ịgba ntụtụ).                                                                                                          |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Megharia ụkpụrụ akara igodo API / nzuzo ama ama nke ọma site na ibu request/response. Ịbanye; enyo `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                                             |

### Azịza-akụkụ: PII Sanitizer

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe               | Ọdabara  | Isi mmalite faịlụ         | Nkọwa                                                                     |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Nyochaa nzaghachi LLM maka PII agbapụtara tupu ịlaghachi na onye ahịa.    |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = nkpuchi PII, `warn` = log naanị, `block` = dobe nzaghachi dum. |

### VS Code Tokenized-Route Context Sanitizer

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                  | Ọdabara | Isi mmalite faịlụ                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`     | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Mwepu ọnọdụ onye editọ na-arụsi ọrụ ike (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) site na arịrịọ `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` wee megharịa ọdịnaya nke faịlụ nwere mmetụta agbakwunyere nke ọma. Nchekwa-na-ndabere; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ. |

### Ihe atụ

| Ihe atụ              | Nhazi                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **nnabata ụlọ ọrụ**  | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (ngbochi injection + request/response PII redaction; ụdịdị nwere onwe) |
| **Nleba anya naanị** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — ndekọ ma ọ dịghị ihe mgbochi                                                                                                          |
| **Ojiji nkeonwe**    | Hapụ — efu niile nwere nkwarụ n'elu                                                                                                                                                                 |

---

## 6. Ngwá ọrụ & Usoro ụzọ

| Mgbanwe                                                     | Ndabara                      | Faịlụ Isi                                             | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                   | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                               | Na-achịkwa ohere LLM nwere iji ngwaọrụ/ịkpọ ọrụ. `allowlist` = naanị ngwaọrụ ndị e depụtara, `denylist` = ngwaọrụ niile ewezuga ndị e depụtara, `disabled` = enweghị mmachibido.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json` | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`                   | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ JSON nke iwu nhazi payload (mmezi upstream dabere n’ụdị model/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                       | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`                   | Oge etiti nbugharị (ms) maka ibugharị faịlụ iwu payload ozugbo. Nke kacha nta bụ `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                          | Nhọrọ ịgbalite: na-esi n’aka ndị ahịa Claude Code duru model ID `claude-*` na-enweghị prefix site na akaụntụ Claude Code OAuth kama ịrịọ prefix onye na-enye ọrụ. Prefix onye na-enye ọrụ doro anya ka na-enwe mmeri. A pụkwara ịhazi ya site na toggle dashboard dị na ibe onye na-enye ọrụ Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                       | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts`       | Tọọ `false` iji kwụsị OmniRoute ịgbakwunye Anthropic beta `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` n’onwe ya (thinking blocks nwere naanị mbinye aka efu). Arịrịọ nwere `thinking.display: "summarized"` na-amapụ ya n’agbanyeghị; beta redact onye ahịa kparịtara ma ọ bụ opt-in `requestDefaults.redactThinking` dakọtara na CC na-eme ka ọ dịgide. Arịrịọ Claude OAuth na-edobe beta ahụ (ọ bụ akụkụ nke akara mkpịsị aka Claude Code OAuth) belụsọ ma feature flag `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED`, nke ndabara ya bụ gbanyụọ, dị n’ọrụ.                                                                  |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                          | Àgwà ochie `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` (#4825): jiri `defaultModel` nke njikọ ahọpụtara dochie aha model ọ bụla na-enweghị prefix. Ọ na-adịkwaghị n’ọrụ na ndabara; naanị `auto` ma ọ bụ model efu ka a na-edochi, ọ na-edekwa ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ugboro maka ụzọ abụọ ọ bụla mgbe ọ dị n’ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                          | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`                    | Oke concurrent kwa model maka combo round-robin (#9100). A kpara semaphore nke combo round-robin ike na arịrịọ concurrent 3 kwa model, na-enweghị override, nke na-eme ka okporo ụzọ nwere concurrency dị elu chere n’azụ oke ahụ. A kwadoro ya ka ọ bụrụ `>= 1`, ma kpachie ya na `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_COMBO_MAX_DEADLINE_FAILURES`                     | `0`                          | `open-sse/services/combo/comboPredicates.ts`          | Oke a na-ahọrọ maka ọsọ combo ọ bụla n'ihe gbasara ọdịda `504` nwere `error.code=STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT` tupu ihe omume mbụ na-abụghị ping. Enweghị ntọala ma ọ bụ `0` na-agbanyụ oke ahụ. Mgbe e ruru oke, a na-eweghachi `504`. Ọdịda ndị ọzọ na EOF bịara n'oge anaghị agụnye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENTIC_STALL_FAILOVER`                          | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/combo/agenticStallFailover.ts`     | Nhọrọ ịgbalite: tọọ ya na `1` (ma ọ bụ `true`) ka onye otu combo nke zara ntụgharị nsonaazụ-tool site na `finish: stop` + nkọwa + oku-tool efu wee gafee gaa na ebumnuche na-esote (atụmatụ priority-loop, round-robin na pinned sessions). Ọ bụrụ na ọ nweghị ebumnuche na-arụpụta nke ka mma, a ga-eweghachi azịza ahụ kwụsịrị kama njehie, a gaghị akwụkwa ụgwọ maka provider breaker ma ọ bụ model lockout. A na-ejide ntụgharị ndị a na-ezipụ site na stream n’aka onye ahịa ruo mgbe a kwụsịrị ikpe ahụ. A naghị ekewa arịrịọ na-eji ọdịdị `input` nke Responses API. Ndabara bụ OFF.                     |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                       | Nhọrọ dị ize ndụ ịgbalite nke na-amapụ nlele mpaghara OmniRoute maka context-window / max-input-token maka arịrịọ kpọmkwem nke otu model. Ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị n’elu ka na-amanye oke ha n’ezie; mkpakọ prompt na oke output-token nke model n’onwe ya ka na-arụ ọrụ. Usoro ụzọ dị irè bụ Feature Flags DB override > environment variable > default; ọ dịghị mkpa ịmalitegharịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`                | Akwụkwọ YAML `providers:` dị n’ime (RIC-738, D4). Mgbe edobere ya (ma ọ nwere ma ọ bụ enweghị ngọngọ `strategy:`), `/v1/chat/completions` na-atụgharị gaa na unified OpenAI-compatible entry nke self-hosted kama pipeline cloud. Mgbe edoghị ya (ndabara), route ahụ na-aga ozugbo na pipeline cloud dị adị. Lee `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`                | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ YAML nwere otu akwụkwọ `providers:` ahụ dị na `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, maka deployments na-ahọrọ faịlụ kama inline env var. Nke ọ bụla n’ime ha na-arụ ọrụ self-hosted entry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(unset — open route)_       | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`                | API key nkekọrịta nhọrọ maka unified self-hosted entry (D5 scaffold, echekwara maka usoro per-key quota). Mgbe edobere ya, arịrịọ ga-enwerịrị `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Mgbe edoghị ya: route ahụ ghe oghe, nke kwekọrọ n’ụkpụrụ self-hosted local-provider dị adị (loopback/trusted-network deployment).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`              | Akwụkwọ YAML `strategy:` dị n’ime maka injin routing deterministic (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, cooldown breaker, cost-priority, latency-aware, fallback chain. Ọ na-ekpuchi ngọngọ `strategy:` dị n’ime `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, kwa-key. Lee `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`              | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ YAML nwere otu akwụkwọ `strategy:` ahụ dị na `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, maka deployments na-ahọrọ faịlụ kama inline env var.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`            | Tọọ `1` iji kwụsị ịnakọta akụkọ mkparịta ụka. `resolveConversationId()` na-eweghachite nsonaazụ a na-adịghị eso tracking tupu ọ gụọ SQLite ma ọ bụ nyochaa akụkọ ozi, a na-ekpuchikwa session IDs ndị client nyere. Ọ dịghị mgbanwe n’otú a si edozi routing-session, a naghị ehichapụkwa records dị adị. Maka deployments na-adịghị eji nlele mkparịta ụka nke dashboard ma chọọ ka tebụl turn kwụsị ịba ụba.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(edoghị atọhapụ)_           | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`        | Igodo API maka atụmatụ router auto-combo `nadir` a na-ahọrọ iji, a na-eji ya mgbe `config.nadir.apiKey` nke combo ahụ n'onwe ya edoghị. Nhọrọ: oku na-enweghị igodo na-adaba na ọkwa anonymous nke Nadir, nke a na-amachibidoro ọnụego ya dabere na IP, ya mere ọ naghị arụ ọrụ n'azụ gateway a na-ekekọrịta. Ọ bụ ihe nwere mmetụta; họrọ ubi nke combo ọ bụla kama mgbe combos na-eji akaụntụ dị iche iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`   | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`        | Ebe mmalite nke API mkpebi Nadir, a na-eji ya mgbe `config.nadir.baseUrl` nke combo ahụ n'onwe ya edoghị. A chọrọ ya naanị maka Nadir a na-akwado n'onwe ya, nke na-edobe isi prompt n'ime netwọk nke gị. A na-anabata ma wepụ `/v1` nke dị n'azụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`                  | Window kwa izu maka oke ojiji USD kwa igodo API (mmegide arịrịọ, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: izu ahụ na-akwụsị na nrụpụta kwa izu kacha mbụ n'etiti njikọ upstream ndị igodo ahụ nwere ike iru, ma ọ bụ bụrụ ụbọchị 7 na-atụgharị mgbe amaghị nke ọ bụla. `calendar`: izu mpaghara site na Mọnde 00:00 ruo Mọnde na-esote 00:00, otu ihe ahụ maka igodo niile. A na-atụ aro `calendar` mgbe otu ọdọ mmiri nke akaụntụ upstream na-ekekọrịta site n'ọtụtụ igodo nwere mmefu ego nke ọ bụla. Uru na-adịghị mma na-alaghachi na `provider`.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(timezone usoro)_           | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`                  | Mpaghara oge IANA (dịka `Asia/Shanghai`) nke na-akọwa Mọnde 00:00 maka window kwa izu `calendar`. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ma ọ bụ na ọ dịghị mma: timezone nke usoro OmniRoute (the `TZ` nke host ma ọ bụ container, nke na-abụkarị nke edoghị na onyonyo container, ya bụ Coordinated Universal Time). A na-eleghara ya anya na ọnọdụ `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                        | Oke ndabara nke ọnụ ọgụgụ tools a na-ekwe ka ọ dị n'arịrịọ (ndabara OpenAI). Naanị integer dị mma; uru ndị edoghị ma ọ bụ na-adịghị mma na-edobe `128`. Provider nwere oke ama ama/na-arụsi ọrụ ike (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) ka na-etinye oke nke ya — nke a na-emegharị naanị elu oke ndabara. Target ndị dị n'ụdị Claude enweghị oke ndabara (Messages API enweghị nke ọ bụla); oke a mụtara ka na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_COUNT`                     | `16`                         | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts`     | Maka ntụgharị Chat: azịza ole nke onyonyo tool-output (`function_call_output`, `custom_tool_call_output`, role `tool`) a ga-ezighachi dị ka akụkụ onyonyo onye ọrụ n'arịrịọ ọ bụla. A na-edobe ndị kacha ọhụrụ, nke ọ bụla ochie karịa ha aghọọ ihe nnọchi ederede nwere `tool_call_id` ya. Naanị integer dị mma; uru ndị edoghị ma ọ bụ na-adịghị mma na-edobe `16`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `20971520` (20 MB)           | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts`     | Ngụkọta nha URL onyonyo tool-output a na-ezighachi n'arịrịọ ọ bụla n'otu ntụgharị ahụ. A na-atụfu onyonyo gafere mmefu ego site na ndị kacha ochie nke mbụ, yana ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ugboro kwa arịrịọ. Naanị integer dị mma; uru ndị edoghị ma ọ bụ na-adịghị mma na-edobe `20971520`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_RESPONSES_STRICT_TOOL_HISTORY`                   | `false`                      | `open-sse/translator/helpers/responsesToolHistory.ts` | Otu esi ejikwa akụkọ ihe mere eme nzaghachi na-ekwekọghị ekwekọ: mmepụta na-enweghị oku, oku na-enweghị mmepụta, na-efu, ogologo oge ma ọ bụ `call_id` na-adịghị mma, ma ọ bụ arụmụka akụkọ ihe mere eme adịghị mma. Gbanyụọ, OmniRoute na-arụzi akụkọ ihe mere eme wee zipu ya (mpụta nke ụmụ mgbei na-adaba, oku a na-azaghị na-enweta ihe efu, ids na-agbanye ma ọ bụ kpọchie, arụmụka na-adịghị mma na-aghọ `{}`). Na, arịrịọ ahụ na-enweta `400` tupu izipu; ezi akụkọ ihe mere eme na-eji ids oku ya na arụmụka JSON zigara ya dịka ezipu ya. Nabata `true` ma ọ bụ `1`; uru ọ bụla ọzọ na-edobe ndabara. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(gbanwuru)_                 | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`                     | Oku tools nke prompt-emulated nke SYNTX.ai: ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya ma ọ bụ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes`, ọ na-etinye katalọgụ tools mpaghara n'ime prompt; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (dịka `0`) na-amafe itinye ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

---

## 7. URLs & Cloud mmekọrịta

| Mgbanwe gburugburu                          | Ndabara                                                         | Faịlụ Isi                                                                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                                  | `http://localhost:20128`                                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | URL -n'akụkụ nkesa maka ọrụ mmekọrịta ime ka ịkpọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a dị ka loopback/container URL ọbụlagodi mgbe ngwa ahụ bụ proxied ọha. Nka A2A na-ejikwa ya (lee `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`) wee daa kama izipu igodo API gaa na http URL ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLOUD_URL`                                 | _(ọ tọgbọ)_                                                     | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | URL njedebe relay igwe ojii (atụmatụ akwụ ụgwọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `12000`                                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Oge nkwụsị HTTP maka arịrịọ mmekọrịta igwe ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                   | `full`                                                          | Nhazi owuwu Webpack                                                         | Profaịlụ n'oge owuwu (tọọ ya na `minimal` iji wepụ modul nwere ikike n'ụzọ anụ ahụ na ngwugwu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                  | _.build/ nke kwụụrụ onwe ya pụta_                               | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`                                     | Mwepụta oge wuo maka ndekọ ndekọ mmepụta nke kwụụrụ onwe ya site na nzọụkwụ nrụgharị nrụpụta gachara. Ọ bụghị nhazi oge ojiri gaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIST_DIR`                        | `dist/` (mgbọrọgwụ repo)                                        | `scripts/build/prepublish.ts`                                               | Ndochi n'oge owuwu maka ndekọ nke nzọụkwụ mbipụta (`prepublish.ts`) na-ede ngwugwu CLI n'ime ya, kama `dist/` dị n'okpuru mgbọrọgwụ repo. A na-ehichapụ ndekọ ahụ tupu owuwu amalite. Ọ bụghị ntọala oge ịgba ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`               | _(empty)_                                                       | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Nzuzo nkekọrịta a na-eji nyochaa mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi Cloud Sync.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`              | `false`                                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka njedebe Cloud Sync dochie nzere mpaghara. Ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE`    | `false`                                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji jụ nzaghachi Cloud Sync na-enweghị mbinye aka mgbe ahazighị nzuzo mpaghara (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka ọ bụla dị — a na-ajụkwa ya mgbe niile mgbe `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` edoghị — n’agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a. Ndabara ga-amalite ịmanye nke a na v3.9.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`      | `false`                                                         | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`                                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji laghachi n’omume v3.8.5 nke otu nzọụkwụ “import everything” na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                      | `http://localhost:20128`                                        | OAuth, Dashboard, sync                                                      | URL ọha a na-ahụ anya maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, na URL ọha ndị emepụtara. Tọọ nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsie ike mgbe oku nloghachi OAuth ma ọ bụ njikọ nchọgharị ndị emepụtara ga-eji aha nnabata reverse-proxy bụ́ canonical.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                     | _(empty)_                                                       | Client-side                                                                 | Ntụgharị mirror dị n’akụkụ onye ahịa nke `CLOUD_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                       | _(unset)_                                                       | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`                                 | Nhọrọ nloghachi ochie maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                          | _(unset — falls back to `PORT`)_                                | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                     | Port nloghachi dị n’akụkụ onye ahịa maka URL ngosi mgbe amaghị origin (SSR/tests); a na-agụ ya tupu `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                 | _(unset)_                                                       | Public-origin resolver, image URLs                                          | Onye na-achọpụta public-origin kacha mkpa maka ihe nchọgharị, nke a na-eji emepụta URL ọha na nyochaa origin ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard. Tọọ nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ na-erute OmniRoute site na URL dị n'ime, mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-eji nweta media emepụtara site na LAN, tunnel, ma ọ bụ origin ọha. E tinyela `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`           | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`                              | URL zuru ezu nke provider plugin manifest a na-akpọsa nye ndị ahịa sidecar. Mgbe edoghị ya, OmniRoute na-enweta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na origin arịrịọ ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`                 | `http`                                                          | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`                              | Protocol a na-eji emepụta URL provider plugin manifest site na HOST/PORT mgbe origin arịrịọ adịghị. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `https` n'azụ public proxy na-akwụsị TLS mgbe edoghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                     | _(unset)_                                                       | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`                                         | Ụdị ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka headers public-origin e bufere. Mgbe edoghị ya = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` obi maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` na-atụkwasị host/proto e bufere obi naanị site na loopback proxy nwere token. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa ndị proxy nọ na private-LAN obi, na-ahapụ bridge `API_PORT` ka ọ debe `X-Forwarded-For` nke onye dị otú ahụ, ma na-ahapụ routes LOCAL_ONLY ịnakwere client nọ na private network nke proxy dị otú ahụ kọrọ (ọ dịghị mgbe ọ bụ arịrịọ Cloudflare-tunnel, chain loopback-only, ma ọ bụ loopback-only CLI-token gate). Họrọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` doro anya na production.                                                           |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                          | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                                                 | URL ọha callback maka ọrụ kie.ai na-arụ n'ụzọ asynchronous. Ọ bụ override kacha mkpa tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                                                 | Ụzọ ọzọ e si ede `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. Ọ na-eji ya eme fallback mgbe edoghị variable mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                      | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                                                 | Origin ọha a na-eji chịkọta URL callback asynchronous.
