Ọ bụ fallback kacha ala maka callback kie.ai; a na-ejikwa ya dịka URL ọha izugbe maka relay ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`                  | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Endpoint nyocha oke CrofAI nke ibe Usage na-eji. Gbanwee ya maka relay / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLINEPASS_USAGE_URL`             | `https://api.cline.bot/api/v1/users/me/plan/usage-limits`       | `open-sse/services/usage/clinepass.ts`                                      | Endpoint oke ClinePass nke awa 5 / kwa izu / kwa ọnwa nke ibe Ojiji na-eji. Gbanwee ya maka relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`              | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`                           | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`                                 | Endpoint ojiji OpenCode Go nke API key na-akwado n’ụzọ gọọmentị, nke ibe Ojiji na-eji. Gbanwee ya maka relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`           | `true`                                                          | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                                            | Mepụta headers njirimara OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUID arịrịọ/session) maka arịrịọ opencode-go/zen upstream nke client eziteghị, ka Cloudflare dị na VPS egress wee nabata ha (#6210/#5997). Ọ na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara kemgbe #10571; jiri `false`/`0`/`no`/`off` kwụsị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                       | `opencode/1.18.31`                                              | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`                                         | User-Agent ndabara a na-eji mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị na mgbe enweghị mgbanwe `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` nke onye na-enye ọrụ. A na-etinye ya naanị na opencode executors. Mgbe arịrịọ na-enweghị key na-akpalite mgbochi upstream, a na-eji uru ahaziri nke na-enweghị `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` dochie ya site na ndabara a kama ịjụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARD_ATTRIBUTION`             | `1` (on)                                                        | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`                                         | Zipu headers njirimara ngwa client (`x-session-id`, `x-title`, `http-referer`) gaa n’aka ndị na-enye ọrụ upstream, ka arịrịọ malitere n’ime harness e depụtara wee debe njirimara ya site na router (OpenRouter publisher gates na-eji ndị a eme mkpebi). `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` na-echekwa njirimara registry/operator ma tụfuo ụkpụrụ ndị client nyere. Headers omenala operator haziri na-emeri ụkpụrụ ndị client nyere mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                           | `desktop`                                                       | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                                            | Uru maka header `x-opencode-client` a na-emepụta mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị na.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                          | `global`                                                        | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                                            | Uru maka header `x-opencode-project` a na-emepụta mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị na.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`        | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`                                     | Ndị na-enye ọrụ upstream ndị ọdịda ha na-ekewa site na egress IP (429 otu na-eme ka njikọ nwanne niile na-ekekọrịta IP ahụ kwụsị). Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, a na-eji ndabara free-tier `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` na-akwụsị cascade ahụ kpamkpam; ndepụta kewara site na rikoma na-anọchi set ahụ. Atụmatụ akwụgoro ụgwọ na-ekewa upstream site na akaụntụ, ya mere enwere ike ịkwụsị ya n’enweghị nsogbu (#9611).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_QUOTA_ROTATION_PROVIDERS` | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`                                     | Oke ntụgharị egress maka ịjụ quota. Mgbe edozighị ya, ọ na-ewepụta onye otu pool n'akụkụ naanị mgbe 429 mere maka ezinụlọ IP-bucketed dị n'elu; `all` na-agbasa ide a nye provider niile, nke mere na rate limit sitere n'aka onye otu na-eme ka nhọrọ ọzọ na-esote pụọ site na egress ọzọ. A na-eleghara uru ọ bụla na-abụghị `all` anya. Ọ chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED` (nke na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara). A na-ahazi store ahụ site na adreesị egress, ọ bụghị site na (provider, address), ya mere 429 gbasaa na-adọba onye otu ahụ maka provider niile ruo mgbe oge ahụ gafere (nkeji 5, na-abawanye ruo nkeji 15). A na-agụ ya maka nsonaazụ nzaghachi ọ bụla, yabụ mgbanwe na-arụ ọrụ n'enweghị ịmalitegharị. |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`       | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts`                            | Tọọ ya na `off` iji kwụsị imezi ahụ́ nke arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo (ọkọlọtọ streaming na ndepụta ngwaọrụ). A ka na-etinye headers. A na-agụ ya maka arịrịọ ọ bụla, yabụ mgbanwe na-arụ ọrụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`      | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts`                            | Aha ngwaọrụ ndị comma kewara ekewa nke a ga-ekwupụta na arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo nke na-enweghị ngwaọrụ ọ bụla, mgbe a hụbeghị ihe ọ bụla maka model ahụ. Ọ bụrụ na ọ bụ efu, ọ na-alaghachi n'otu placeholder nke a gwara model ahụ ka ọ ghara ịkpọ. Ọ gaghị akarị ndenye 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; a na-eleghara ndenye na-adịghị mma anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_CREDENTIAL`             | `public`                                                        | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts`                            | Credential a na-eziga na free-tier lane nwere mgbochi (`https://opencode.ai/inference/v1`). Kemgbe 2026-10-21, lane ahụ na-aza 403 `Model access is disabled` nye igodo API dị adị ma kama na-eje ozi literal na-enweghị aha. `key` na-edobe igodo API onye ọrụ, `none`/`off` anaghị eziga credential header, ebe a na-eziga literal ọ bụla ọzọ dịka bearer token. A na-agụ ya maka arịrịọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_RELAY_URL`              | ịtọhapụ                                                         | `src/lib/opencodeFreeRelay.ts`                                              | Ntọala URL nke Bun relay maka arịrịọ OpenCode na-akwụghị ụgwọ. Mgbe edobere, arịrịọ ndị ahụ na-ebu ngwa ngwa ngwa ngwa usoro OpenCode CLI na ntọala ngwaọrụ wee ziga ya site na relay (`scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`), n'ihi na elu na-aza 429 nye ndị ahịa na-abụghị Bun HTTP. A na-amalite Loopback URLs na ihe nkesa mgbe `bun` dị na PATH; a na-eleghara ụzọ na URL anya. A na-anabata `http://` dị larịị naanị maka ndị ọbịa loopback (`localhost`, `127.0.0.0/8`, `::1`); onye ọbịa ọ bụla ga-eji `https://`, ma ọ bụghị ya, arịrịọ na-ada na njehie doro anya. Naanị mmecha nkata. Gụọ kwa arịrịọ.                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_BUN`                   | `bun` na PATH                                                   | `src/lib/opencodeFreeRelay.ts`                                              | ọnụọgụ abụọ Bun na-eji malite relay.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_SCRIPT`                | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts` n'okpuru mgbọrọgwụ ngwugwu | `src/lib/opencodeFreeRelay.ts`                                              | Ụzọ nke edemede relay. A na-edozi ihe ndabara site na mgbọrọgwụ ngwugwu arụnyere, mgbe ahụ ndekọ ndekọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_HOST`                  | `127.0.0.1`                                                     | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | Relay listen host.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_PORT`                  | `20199`                                                         | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | Relay ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_UPSTREAM`              | `https://opencode.ai`                                           | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | Malite relay na-aga n'ihu. Kwesịrị ịbụ `https://` (`http://` naanị maka mmalite ule loopback); relay jụrụ ịmalite ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_TOKEN`                 | ịtọhapụ                                                         | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`, `open-sse/executors/opencodeRelay.ts` | Ekekọrịtara nzuzo. OmniRoute na-eziga ya na `x-opencode-relay-auth`; relay na-aza 401 na-enweghị ya. Achọrọ: relay nwere `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_HOST` na-abụghị loopback jụrụ ịmalite na-enweghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_PROXY_ALLOWLIST`       | ịtọhapụ                                                         | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | `host` ma ọ bụ `host:port` egress nkewapụrụ rịkọm na-anabata relay na-abụghị loopback na-anabata ndị na-akpọ oku (`x-opencode-relay-proxy`); ọ bụla ọzọ proxy zara 403. Ignored on a loopback bind.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`          | `https://ollama.com/settings`                                   | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | URL ntọala Ollama Cloud a na-eji achịkọta ozi quota. Gbanwee ya maka relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_BALANCE_URL`        | `https://ollama.com/api/balance`                                | `open-sse/services/ollamaCloudBalance.ts`                                   | Endpoint balance nke Ollama Cloud upstream depụtara (`GET https://ollama.com/api/balance`, `Authorization: Bearer <API key>`). A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe module na-ebulite. A na-eji ya mbụ maka quota Ollama Cloud mgbe njikọ ahụ nwere igodo API; onye na-anakọta cookie nke ibe ntọala ka na-abụ ụzọ ndabere. Gbanwee ya maka relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                       | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Cookie `__Secure-session` nke Ollama Cloud a na-eji achịkọta quota site na ibe ntọala. Ọ bụ ozi nwere mmetụta; họrọ ubi Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahazichara ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`                 | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Env var cookie `__Secure-session` ọzọ nke Ollama Cloud. Ọ bụ ozi nwere mmetụta; họrọ ubi Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahazichara ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`             | _(unset)_                                                       | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Kuki `__Secure-session` ọzọ nke Ollama Cloud, nke a na-eji env var tupu aliases ndị dị mkpụmkpụ. Ọ bụ ozi nwere mmetụta; ọ ka mma iji mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`          | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`                 | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Endpoint oke ojiji CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Gbanwee ya maka relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebugharị n'azụ proxy (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` na URL ọha gị kwụsiri ike (dịka, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe OAuth akpọghachite ma ọ bụ njikọ ọha ewepụtara ga-eji aha nnabata ahụ. Enweghị nke a, ịkpọghachi OAuth nwere ike ịda n'ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adaba na njikọ ọhaneze ewepụtara nwere ike tụọ aka na mmalite akpa.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` dị ka ime loopback/container URL maka ọrụ nkesa-na-sava. Ejila `Origin` ihe nchọgharị ma ọ bụ aha nnabata ọhaneze maka ihe njiri mara nke ime onwe ya.
>
> Akwụkwọ dashboard enwetara nke ọma anaghị achọ ebe ọhaneze kwụ ọtọ URL: dashboard ahụ na-eziga arịrịọ na-adịghị mma site na iji akara CSRF nwere oge. OmniRoute ka na-emezi nkwado ndabere nke ọha maka njikọta ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard: a tụkwasịrị ntụkwasị obi ọha URL env vars mbụ; A na-eleghara isi okwu `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` raw ma ọ bụrụ na enyere `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` aka yana akara ndị ọgbọ proxy ozugbo dị ka ntụkwasị obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie arịrịọ dashboard nke sitere na otu; CORS bụ naanị maka ndị ahịa ihe nchọgharị gafere.

---

## 8. Proxy na-apụ apụ

Ndị na-eweta LLM na-akpọ ụzọ elu site na HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 proxy maka njikwa egress, geo-routing, ma ọ bụ IP masking.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` dabara na `true`. Tọọ ya na `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ `off` ịmanye HTTP/1.1 na ndị na-ezigara Undici na `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts` na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: kpọmkwem egress, HTTP(S) /SOCKS proxies, relays, na ha ọhụrụ-socket retries. Nke a na-echekwa nkwenye asambodo TLS, nyocha proxy, oke njikọ yana ntọala idobe ndụ. Malitegharịa OmniRoute mgbe ịgbanwere ya n'ihi na a na-echekwa ndị na-ezipụ ozi na-emepụta ndị ọrụ relay na mmalite. Mgbanwe ahụ anaghị ejikwa akara mkpịsị aka TLS wreq ma ọ bụ ụgbọ njem akọwapụtara nke onye na-eweta ya.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                       | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`       | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kwado onye nnọchi anya SOCKS5 maka oku elu. Wepụ na `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_GZIP_REQUEST_BODIES`          | `true`       | `open-sse/executors/base/gzipRequestBody.ts`   | Gzip ahụ arịrịọ ezigara `api.anthropic.com` gọọmentị (mkpụrụedemede + 4096, naanị njikọ kpọmkwem; mafere n'azụ proxy, iṅomi TLS ma ọ bụ `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`). Anthropic na-anabata gzip naanị. Ntinye koodu jụrụ (415, 400/403 na-abụghị Anthropic, ma ọ bụ mmalite JSON-parse 400) na-ewe iwe na-enweghị mgbako otu ugboro na mmalite ya na-anọkwa n'enweghị mgbako ruo otu elekere. Wepụ na `0`, `false`, `off` ma ọ bụ `no`. |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`       | Ndị ahịa-akụkụ                                 | Mmata n'akụkụ ndị ahịa maka nnweta SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`       | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara na-ndabere (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB meriri emerie). Ọdọ mmiri proxy na ntụgharị akaụntụ ọ bụla kwụsịrị ijere onye otu nke dara ada (ajụjụ TCP nyocha, ma ọ bụ 429 site na ya) maka oge na-abawanye ugboro abụọ na nkwugharị ọ bụla, ruo okpu. `false` weghachite nhọrọ dị larịị.                                                                                              |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Nye ndepụta ohere maka ebumnuche njikwa mpaghara-isi nke gafere loopback (comma/space kewapụrụ ndị ọbịa ma ọ bụ IPs, `*` na-emepe LAN/private mana ọ dịghị mgbe metadata/link-local). A na-anabataghachi azụ azụ http/https na ndabara. Uru dashboard DB na-emeri mgbanwe a mgbe edobere na enweghị isi.                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`     | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oge ntọala (ms) nke 429 ewepụtara, gbagoro okpukpu abụọ na nkwugharị ọ bụla. Gụọ otu ugboro na mmalite. Akpọrọ ya na `1000..3600000`; na-abụghị oke ma ọ bụ enweghị ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara na ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`     | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Okpu (ms) nke 429 set-side. Gụọ otu ugboro na mmalite. Akpọrọ ya na `1000..3600000`; na-abụghị oke ma ọ bụ enweghị ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara na ịdọ aka ná ntị. Mgbe okpu dị n'okpuru ntọala, ọ na-adaghachi na ndabara. Iweda okpu na-ebelata ogologo oge ichefu: ndenye na-ehichapụ `2 x max` ka ọ gwụchara.                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`      | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). Maka ndị na-eweta oke nke adreesị egress kpuchiri, họpụta onye otu ọdọ mmiri na-ekerịta adreesị egress onye otu jụrụ nso nso a dị n'okpuru ndị otu ahụike. Naanị ịtụ, ewepughị ya. Chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, nke na-emepụta mgbama ọjụjụ ọ na-agụ.                                                                 |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`     | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Window nlọghachi (ms) capping kwugharịa `proxy.set_aside` webhook alerts kwa onye otu ọdọ mmiri ka mwapu nke jụrụ na-eziga otu mmemme. Gụọ na nke ọ bụla emit. Akpọrọ ya na `1000..3600000`; na-anọghị nso ma ọ bụ enweghị ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara na-agba nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | HTTP proxy maka oku elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | HTTPS proxy maka oku elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | Proxy zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Na-atụnye ebumnuche echo-IP nke proxy egress probes ji mee otu URL. Atọghaghị, nyocha ahụ na-anwale `api64.ipify.org` wee `api4.ipify.org` yabụ na ọwara IPv4-only anwụghị (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | hostnames/IPs kewapụrụ rikoma ka ịgafe proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`         | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Oke sọks na-emekọ ihe n'otu onye na-ezipụ HTTP/SOCKS proxy nke echekwara. iyi SSE dị ogologo dị ka Codex `/v1/responses` chọrọ njikọ karịa otu mgbe ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-ekekọrịta otu proxy ọkwa akaụntụ. Uru dị n'elu `256` ka agbachiela.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`      | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | SOCKS5 aka aka (jikọọ) akwụsịla na ms. Welie ya mgbe otu nnukwu ọnụ ụzọ ámá nwere otu onye ọbịa (dịka ọmụmaatụ 100 arịrịọ n'otu oge) — ezigbo mmanye aka nwere ike gafere 10s n'okpuru ọdọ mmiri juru eju n'agbanyeghị na enwere ike iru proxy, nke na-apụta dị ka `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable` ụgha. Edebere na `120000`.                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`      | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Mgbe `false` (ndabara), arịrịọ onye proxy ekenyere na-emezughị bụ** jụrụ (emechiri emechi)** kama ịdaghachi azụ na njikọ kpọmkwem — na-egbochi ezigbo ntapu IP. Tọọ `true` iji weghachi azụ azụ azụ DIRECT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`      | `open-sse/executors`                           | Mpempe mkpịsị TLS na-eji wreq-js (na-eṅomi Chrome 124). Mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(Edobeghi)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Ndepụta ohere ndị na-eweta rịkọm maka ụzọ TLS proxied ọhụrụ (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). Direct TLS na-edobe omume ihe nketa ya mgbe edobere ya; naanị ndị na-enye ndị a na-aga site na akwa akara mkpịsị aka Chrome-124. ewepu Groq (`api.groq.com`) ọbụlagodi mgbe edobere listi ikike, n'ihi na Chrome JA3 na-ebute Cloudflare 1010 ebe undici nwa afọ ruru API.                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`      | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Kwe ka ọnọdụ ihe nchọgharị Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie akwụkwọ HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Ihe atụ

| Ihe atụ                                       | Nhazi                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site n'ọwara SSH**                   | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Ụlọ ọrụ HTTP proxy**                        | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Mgbochi mkpịsị aka**                        | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — chọrọ `wreq-js` (gụnyere)                                                                 |
| **Egress na-achịkwa / enweghị ohere ozugbo**  | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (nke ndabara). Arịrịọ na-ada nke ọma mgbe proxy adịghị adị kama isite n'aka ozugbo.          |
| **Legacy/ dev — kwe ka ndaghachi azụ ozugbo** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachi omume tupu ikesi ike: njikọ a na-eji mgbe mkpebi proxy dara.                         |

> **Mara (NVIDIA nkwado uzo — #3226):** NVIDIA API-igodo nkwado nkwado njedebe mgbe a chụpụrụ ya na proxy/TLS-patched zuru ụwa ọnụ (ndi na-ezipụ → 504). `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` kpachaara anya na-agafe patch proxy maka otu oku nkwado ahụ site na iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`. Nke a bụ akwụkwọ edebere, wezuga oke — ọ na-eme** ọ bụghị** emetụta chat/usage egress. Eji `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` kechie uzo ngafe ahụ.

## 9. Njikọ Ngwá Ọrụ CLI

Na-achịkwa ka OmniRoute si achọpụta ma malite ụzọ ụgbọ ala CLI ( Koodu Claude, Codex, wdg).

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                | Ọdabara                                                 | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                                                  | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | `auto` = usoro ọchụchọ PATH; `manual` = jiri naanị ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                                            | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ndenye PATH agbakwunyere maka nchọpụta ọnụọgụ abụọ CLI (nkewapụrụ n'ọnụ ọnụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                                            | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Wepụ ndekọ ụlọ maka ịgụ CLI configs (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Ga-abụ nke zuru oke na n'ime usoro a n'ụlọ —** ma ọ bụ** , n'ime akpa, ụzọ a na-ekekọta (nke ahụ bụ otú `/host-home` si arụ ọrụ). Ihe ọ bụla ọzọ na-adaghachi na dir home.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                                                  | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Kwe ka OmniRoute dee faịlụ nhazi CLI (nkwalite ume ọhụrụ, data nnọkọ). Tọọ na `false` iji mee ka nhazi CLI ọ bụla dee daa na mperi "na-ede nkwarụ".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                                                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na Droid CLI ọnụọgụ abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, wee `cursor`                                   | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ onye nnọchite Cursor. Enweghị ya, nchọpụta na-agbalị `agent` na mbụ wee daa azụ na `cursor`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala gaa na ọnụọgụ abụọ CLI 5dive.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_5DIVE_STATE_DIR`             | `/var/lib/5dive`                                        | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | 5dive's system state dir (profaịlụ auth nwere mgbọrọgwụ); mirrors 5dive nke onwe STATE_DIR ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLI_CONTINUE_BIN`                | `cn`                                                    | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala gaa n'ihu CLI ọnụọgụ abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CLI_QODER_BIN`                   | `qodercli`                                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CLI_QWEN_BIN`                    | `qwen`                                                  | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala gaa na koodu Qwen CLI ọnụọgụ abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_AIDER_BIN`                   | `aider`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala gaa na ọnụọgụ abụọ Aider CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Tọọ nke a na 512000 iji mee ka arịrịọ — dị warara, atụmatụ ahụ na-agụ obere ya. A na-eleghara uru ọ bụla ọzọ anya, yabụ typo enweghị ike ịrịọ maka windo ihe nlereanya ahụ enweghị. |

A na-ajụ arịrịọ ka ukwuu na mpaghara yana 413 kama ibuga ya, n'ihi na ọjụjụ ọnụ ụzọ ámá na-emechi ihe nlereanya ahụ na ndaghachi azụ. Ihe ndabara bụ windo gburugburu nke ọnụ ụzọ ámá (1000000); Uru a nwere ike wedata elu ụlọ ahụ, ebulighị ya n'elu windo.                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MINIMAX_CODE_MAX_TOKENS`         | `8192`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/minimaxCodeGateway.ts`            | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke ọnụ ụzọ ámá ejirila nwere ike iji zaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| `MINIMAX_CODE_PROFILE_DIR`        | `~/.minimax`                                            | `open-sse/executors/minimaxCodeGateway.ts`            | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha profaịlụ MiniMax nke jide profaịlụ aut.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Ihe efu na-ahọrọ ọgbọ kachasị ọhụrụ, nke bụ nke CLI n'onwe ya na-enye ume ọhụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MINIMAX_CODE_REGION`             | `en`                                                    | `open-sse/executors/minimaxCodeGateway.ts`            | Mpaghara nke akaụntụ MiniMax. Ọ na-ekpebi ebe njedebe akaụntụ ahụ na-eme ka akara ngosi ahụ na ndekọ profaịlụ na-agụ nzere ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `HERMES_HOME`                     | `~/.hermes`                                             | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/hermesHome.ts`   | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha Hamis Agent ebe OmniRoute reads/writes nhazi Hermes CLI. Dakọtara env var ihe nrụnye Hamis PowerShell na Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
