Mgbe edoghị ya, ọ na-achọta `.whycodes` n'okpuru `HOME`, e mesịa `USERPROFILE`, e mesịa `os.homedir()`. A na-eji uru nnọchi ahụ kpọmkwem dịka e nyere ya; a naghị agbasawanye `~` dị na mbido.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

### CLI Profaịlụ Auto-mmekọrịta

Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ ndị a abanyela na ndabara. Enwere ike ịtụgharị ha na dashboard koodu CLI.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-eweta, na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.codex/*.config.toml` na-akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ndụ. chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; adịghị agbanwe agbanwe active/default Codex config, auth, Codex-lb ntọala, ma ọ bụ onye na-eweta nhọrọ.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Ka emechara mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-eweta, na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` Claude na-akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ndụ. chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; anaghị agbanwe agbanwe active/default Claude config, auth, ma ọ bụ onye na-eweta nhọrọ. |

### Docker Ihe Nlereanya

```bash
# Mount host binaries into the container and tell OmniRoute where they are:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe a na-ejikọta ụzọ ahụ n'ezie site na onye ọbịa — jikọtara ya na ugwu dị ka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee profaịlụ `host` na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ nke na-abụghị n'ime ụlọ ndị na-ebu akpa ma ọ bụ n'elu ugwu, n'ihi na a ga-atụfu ihe odide ebe ahụ mgbe emegharịrị akpa ahụ.

Ihe onyonyo a na-agba dị ka `USER node`, yabụ `/root` enweghị mgbakwasa bụ** ọ bụghị** ezigbo ngafe.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                       | Ọdabara        | Isi mmalite faịlụ                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------- | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(na akpaaka)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Mmanye nchọpụta akpa na (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ (`0`/`false`). Naanị achọrọ na oge ọsọ ọsọ, nchọpụta akpaaka na-efunahụ ya.                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`        | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka nhazi ngwa ngwa CLI na-ede n'ime ụzọ akpa enweghị n'agbanyeghị. Ihe nhata CLI bụ `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                                        |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | ịtọhapụ        | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | naanị foto Docker : dashboard** Melite Ugbu a** na-ede arịrịọ JSON na faịlụ a (ga-abụrịrị n'ime `DATA_DIR`) maka onye na-ahụ maka ndị ọbịa ka ọ dọpụta ma megharịa akpa ahụ. Hụ [ntuziaka Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### CLI Binary (`omniroute`) ndị enyemaka

Ngbanwe ndị a na-emegharị omume ọnụọgụ abụọ `omniroute` CLI (ọ bụghị nchọpụta akụkụ ụgbọ ala dị n'elu).

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara                           | Isi mmalite faịlụ                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(sistemu) _                      | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Kwado asụsụ mmepụta CLI. mpaghara BCP-47 (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Wepụ sistemu mpaghara env vars (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tọọ `1` ka ibugharịa stdout/stderr nkesa na ọnụ na ọnọdụ a na-elekọta. Dakọtara na ọkọlọtọ `--log` na `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Agbara akara nyocha igwe dị ka nkụnye eji isi `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Emepụtara akpaaka na ọrụ 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | mgbọrọgwụ Gemini na-adịgide adịgide; otu agba ọsọ na-eji ụlọ dịpụrụ adịpụ nwa oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Oge amara na milliseconds tupu mmanye-egbu nwa CLI akara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Tọọ na 1 jiri DATA_DIR doro anya ka ịwụpụ faịlụ gburugburu data-akwụkwọ ndekọ aha nke onye ọrụ ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Token mmekọrịta maka `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` na-anabata naanị akara mmekọrịta, ọ bụghị igodo API ). Dakọtara na `--token`.                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | N'otu oge HTTP nkwụsị oge (ms) maka arịrịọ nkesa CLI →.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kachasị (ms) CLI na-echere ebe njedebe ahụike nkesa tupu ibipụta ịdọ aka ná ntị nkwụsị oge. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite oyi nwayọ (dịka Windows). A na-edozikwa site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tọọ na `1` ka ibipụta nchọpụta retry/backoff na stderr n'oge iwu CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Ndekọ ahaziri maka nchọpụta ngwa mgbakwunye CLI (ngwugwu `omniroute-cmd-*` ). Ọ na-adabara `~/.omniroute/plugins/` mgbe ewepụtaghị ya. CLI-naanị — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-erute nyocha ngwa mgbakwunye n'akụkụ ihe nkesa, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-egosi (ngalaba 2).              |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Wepu nlele mmelite mgbe edobere ya na uru ọ bụla na-adịghị efu: ọkwa CLI na ọpụpụ usoro yana ọkọlọtọ “mmelite dị” dashboard (`GET /api/system/version` wee wụpụ npm, GitHub na nlele ozi). Ọkwa CLI na-anọkwa na CI, ọnọdụ dị jụụ, yana mmepụta anaghị ede.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(nke ewepụtara)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | Ndụ URL nke `omniroute doctor` nyochara . Nhọrọ `livenessUrl` mmemme na-ebute ụzọ; ma ọ bụghị ya, a na-eji ọnụ ahịa a ọnụ. Atọghị, CLI na-enweta njedebe `/api/health/degradation` nke dashboard mpaghara.                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Ụzọ katalọgụ onye na-enye iwu nke ndị na-eweta CLI. Nhọrọ `catalogPath` mmemme na-ebute ụzọ. A na-eji ụzọ zuru oke eme ihe dị ka; ikwu ụzọ dozie si oru ngo mgbọrọgwụ. Ewepụghị ya, CLI na-eji akwụkwọ ndekọ aha ndị na-eweta ya, yana faịlụ `providers.ts` nke ketara dị ka ọdịda. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(ndabara onye na-eweta ya)_      | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Ihe nlere ọdịda zuru ụwa ọnụ maka ule njikọ ndị na-eweta CLI. Ahaziri ụkpụrụ njikọ ụzọ ụzọ, ụdị onye na-eweta kpọmkwem gabigara, uru a, emesia ụdị nnwale arụnyere n'ime onye na-eweta ya.                                                                                          |

---

## 10. Internal Agent & MCP Integrations

| Variable                                        | Default                                             | Source File                                                 | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | chọpụta akpaaka                                     | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL doro anya maka ngwaọrụ MCP/A2A iru OmniRoute. Na-ewepụ nchọpụta akpaaka localhost. Nka A2A dara kama izipu igodo API na URL a (ma ọ bụ `BASE_URL` ọ dabara na ya) mgbe ọ dị larịị http gaa na adreesị ọha, aha nnabata ọha nwere ntụpọ ma ọ bụ onye nnabata igwe ojii, ma ọ bụ jide nzere: jiri https, ma ọ bụ http gaa loopback, akpa ma ọ bụ aha ọrụ, ma ọ bụ adreesị nzuzo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | MCP/A2A modulu                                              | Igodo API maka oku akụrụngwa MCP dị n'ime. Nka A2A na-eziga ya naanị maka onye na-akpọ oku na-enweghị igodo API (nnọkọ dashboard); onye na-akpọ oku ji mkpịsị ugodi na-eji nke ya eme ihe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(edobebeghị)_                                      | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | NJirimara igodo maka ịkọwa onye nwe ndekọ nyocha MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(edobebeghị)_                                      | Legacy                                                      | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ọnụọgụ ụbọchị a na-edobe akụkọ ọrụ A2A n’ebe nchekwa data mpaghara tupu nhichapụ kwa ụbọchị ehichapụ ahịrị. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ bụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ `<= 0`, ọ na-alaghachi na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Mgba ọkụ nkwụsị maka njirimara nleba anya nke memory-hits A2A. Tọọ ya na `0` ka ọ ghara ime nyocha ncheta maka ọrụ ahụ kpamkpam; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere mgbe edoghị ya) na-eme ka ọ nọgide na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-eme ka njedebe Issue Agent offline/local recorded-triage rụọ ọrụ. Hapụ ya ka ọ bụrụ nke kwụsịrị belụsọ ma ị na-arụ ọrụ local recorded-triage workflows n’amaghị ama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(unset)_                                           | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge njedebe (ms) maka otu ọsọ Issue Agent recorded-triage. A na-amachibido ya na oke ime ụlọ; ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara e wuru n’ime ya mgbe edobereghị ya ma ọ bụ mgbe uru ya ezighi ezi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(active context)_                                  | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Context/profile remote-mode nke CLI maka iwu `omniroute`; ọ na-emeri context na-arụ ọrụ dị na local contexts store. Ọ dabara na `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Gbanyụọ backend keychain OS nke `keytar` nhọrọ maka nzere context CLI. Mgbe enyere ya, nzere na-anọgide na ọnọdụ `0600` nke `config.json`, CLI na-ezipụkwa ịdọ aka ná ntị fallback otu ugboro; e bu n’obi ya maka ọrụ headless/container a kpachapụrụ anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Manye njikwa nnweta dabere na scope maka oku ngwaọrụ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(all)_                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scopes kewara site na rikoma: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_RESEARCH_ENABLED`               | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/mcp-server/tools/perplexityResearch.ts`           | Debanye ngwaọrụ Perplexity Deep Research MCP. Naanị `1` na-edebanye ya; ihe ọ bụla ọzọ na-ahapụ ya ka ọ kwụsị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Kpọkọta nkọwa ngwaọrụ MCP tupu ị serializie manifest. Uru ndị na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ bụ: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algọridim/profaịlụ mkpakọ. Uru ndị na-agbanyụ ya: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Mmefu oge tupu nkwụsị (ms) maka ọgụgụ njikwa ime MCP-server (ahụike, resilience, ngwakọta, quota, ojiji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Mmefu oge tupu nkwụsị (ms) maka mbufe MCP na-eche onye na-enye ọrụ (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SESSION_IDLE_MS`                 | `300000`                                            | `open-sse/mcp-server/httpTransport.ts`                      | TTL (ms) na-adịghị arụ ọrụ tupu ihicha ahụ aghọrọ nnọkọ MCP na-adịghị arụ ọrụ. A naghị ewe oge ọ bụla nnọkọ nwere iyi mepere emepe ma ọ bụ arịrịọ ụgbọ elu. Ụkpụrụ emeghị nke ọma, ndị na-abụghị ọnụọgụ ma ọ bụ ndị na-adịghị mma na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke ihe atụ mpaghara-corpus index nke echekwara na ebe nchekwa (LRU, otu n'ime ndekọ ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ indexed). Ejidere na opekempe nke `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Oge etiti mmekọrịta katalọgụ model n’ime awa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oge etiti nyocha oke ọsọ na quota nke onye na-enye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oghere (ms) dị n’etiti nbudata OAuth quota abụọ na-esochi n’ime mmekọrịta buru ibu; a na-ebudata njikọ OAuth otu n’otu iji zere izipu arịrịọ buru ibu n’otu oge n’ebe upstream nọ. `0` pụtara ịpụ na nke a (concurrent).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Oge etiti kacha nta (ms) dị n’etiti nbudata upstream quota abụọ na-esochi n’ụzọ preflight/monitor nke arịrịọ ọ bụla; ọ na-etinye oghere n’etiti oku netwọk concurrent ka ọtụtụ akaụntụ dị n’otu IP ghara izipu arịrịọ buru ibu n’ebe upstream nọ. Ejikọtala ya na ndị na-ebudata quota nke Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (saịtị nbudata abụọ ahụ), OpenCode, na Crof (#6009, #6911). Ụzọ izipu `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` izugbe (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) akabeghị ekpuchi — a na-eso ya iche. Cache hits anaghị emetụta ya. `0` na-agbanyụ ya; a na-egbochi ya n’ókè `0..60000`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Oghere random ọzọ kwa arịrịọ (ms), nke a na-ahọpụta mgbe ọ bụla a na-edobe fetch quota upstream. Ọ na-eji 120 eme ihe na ndabara; a na-amachibido ya na `0..60000`; `0` na-ewepụ naanị jitter. Tọọ `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` iji kwụsị throttle niile. Jitter anaghị ekwe nkwa igbochi mmachibido sitere na upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CODEX_QUOTA_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/codexQuotaFetcher.ts`                    | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka otu mkpokọta mkpokọta Codex (`chatgpt.com/backend-api/wham/usage`). Ụgbọ elu na-eche mbute a tupu izipu arịrịọ ahụ. Mgbe oge nkwụsị ma ọ bụ njehie njem gasịrị, a ga-ejigharị foto ikpeazụ site na nkeji iri na ise gara aga ma ọ bụrụ na windo ya agwụla ma ọ bụ tọgharịa; n'enweghị nke ọ bụla, amaghi oke oke ahụ, ya mere a na-amafe akaụntụ kredit akwụ ụgwọ maka arịrịọ ahụ ma ọ nweghị nkwụsịtụ na-adịgide. Na-anabata ọnụọgụ dị mma ruo `2147483647`; uru ọ bụla ọzọ na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                     |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oge echere (ms) tupu emelite provider limits mgbe ihe omume ojiji n'ezie gasịrị, iji nye upstream quota API oge ịdebanye oriri ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | chọpụta akpaaka                                     | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`             | Ụzọ zuru oke na Chrome ma ọ bụ Edge executable ejiri maka ntinye aka na nbanye Adobe Firefly mmekọrịta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`                 | enyere                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-eme ka IMS na steeti ihe ize ndụ ihe nchọgharị dị ọhụrụ yana oge Chrome CDP nwere akaụntụ. Tọọ na `0` iji gbanyụọ mmeghari ihe nchọgharị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                    | enyere                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-aga n'ihu na-arụzi Adobe sessions n'okpuru `DATA_DIR` gafee usoro malitegharịa. Tọọ na `0` ka idobe naanị ebe nchekwa oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`               | `12000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Opekempe kacha nta na millisecond n'etiti Adobe Firefly na-ewepụta ntinye; `0` na-ewepụ oghere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`              | `15000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Ogologo oge dị jụụ na milliseconds ka nrubeisi Adobe nke atọ ọ bụla gachara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`            | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`                   | Base backoff na milliseconds ka nzaghachi Adobe 408 na-agafe agafe; jikọtara ya na nrubeisi ohere gafee na kacha ise mgbalị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEGABRAIN_API_URL`                             | `https://api.getmegabrain.com`                      | `open-sse/services/usage/megabrain.ts`                      | Ntọala URL maka nnọkọ atụmatụ MegaBrain na oke izu ụka na oke ndị na-enye (nke ejiri akara OAuth njikọ ma ọ bụ igodo `mb-v1` API kwadoro); mebie naanị maka mgbanaka ma ọ bụ ihe nrụnye ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES`         | `false`                                             | `src/instrumentation-node.ts`                               | Gbanyụọ ọrụ ndabere niile: mmeghari quota, mmekọrịta provider-limits, mgbake njikọ, ahụike nzere, mmekọrịta igwe ojii/ụdị/proxy efu/ahụike proxy, nhicha retention, VACUUM a haziri oge, na nyocha hot-reload nke ntọala. Ọ bara uru maka CI/test; n’ime nrụnye na-agba ogologo oge, njikọ agaghị agbake na-akpaghị aka, ebe nchekwa data ga-anọgide na-abawanye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEADLESS`                            | _(edobeghị)_                                        | `src/lib/system/headless.ts`                                | Mbido naanị maka proxy-engine: ọ na-awụpụ subsystem nhọrọ, `/dashboard` → 404. Ọ bụ otu ihe ahụ na `omniroute serve --headless`. ### Àkwà Njikọ OAuth CLI (N'ime)

| Variable            | Default     | Source File                     | Description                                     |
| ------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | auto-detect | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL sava maka àkwà njikọ nkwenye CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(unset)_   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token nkwenye maka àkwà njikọ CLI.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`       | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | NJ onye ọrụ maka nnọkọ àkwà njikọ CLI.          |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(unset)_   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.          |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(unset)_   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.           |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(unset)_   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.         |

## 11. OAuth nzere onye na-enye

### Ntugharị akara Codex na-agafe agafe

`OMNIROUTE_TOKEN_REFRESH_PASSIVE_PROVIDERS` atọrọ na `codex` na-eme ihe atụ a ka ọ bụrụ onye na-eji nzere Codex emekọrịtara ọnụ, ọ bụghị onye nwe ntụgharị ntughari ume ọhụrụ. Ihe ndabara efu ma ọ bụ atọkwaghị na-echekwa omume ume ọhụrụ. A na-akpachapụ ụkpụrụ ndị kewara rịkọm na enweghị mmetụta; `cx` bụ utu aha maka `codex`. Ndị na-enye nkwado anaghị akwado nkwado mmalite. Ndị na-enye ndị ọzọ agbanwebeghị.

N'ebe a na-enye ume ọhụrụ dị ugbu a, ihe atụ na-agafe cache njikọ wee nabata naanị ihe dị iche, akara ohere agwụbeghị. Tokens jụrụ elu n'oge arịrịọ ahụ enweghị ike iru eru ọzọ ka mmekọrịta gachara azụ: nzere arịrịọ ahụ na-ejide n'ọtụtụ akara mkpịsị aka SHA-256 anọ, tụfuo ya na arịrịọ ahụ. Naanị oghere akara ka anabatara, yabụ metadata onye na-eweta arịrịọ na-adị ndụ. Ọ nweghị nzere anabatara anabatara. Ọ bụrụ na enweghị ngbanwe dị, `refresh_disabled` abụghị ọnụ ma ọ naghị agụta na mgbaji sekit na-enye ume ọhụrụ. Nzaghachi 401/403 dị n'ezie na-ejigide ndekọ ndekọ ọdịda nkịtị, oyi oyi n'ọnụ na njehie ndị ahịa; akara nbipu akaụntụ ka na-akpọchikwa kpamkpam.

Ntughari dashboard/API nke aka na-eweghachi `409` nwere koodu `refresh_disabled` yana ntuziaka iji nweta ume na ihe atụ na-arụ ọrụ yana mekọrịta. Ntugharị ozugbo (akparịta ụka, ojiji, nyocha ahụike na nkwado mbubata) anaghị egosipụta njehie ahụ; ojiji a na-achọrọ 401 mgbake na-emegharị otu ugboro site na iji akara emekọrịtara anabatara. Mbubata Codex auth na-echekwa akara ngosi enyere na-enweghị mgbanwe mgbanwe ume ọhụrụ; Nbanye OAuth ọhụrụ agbanwebeghị. Ọ nweghị mmekọrịta tupu izipu agbakwunyere na mpụga ụzọ ume ọhụrụ dị adị.

Debe otu onye nwe ntụgharị ma hazie mmekọrịta akara iche iche, tupu ohere akara agwụ (ndụ ụzọ na-arụ ọrụ nke ọma: nkeji ise). Nhọrọ a abụghị mkpọchi ekesara, ntugharị nchekwa data ma ọ bụ nbunye SQLite nkekọrịta. Isi mmalite: `open-sse/services/tokenRefresh/passiveCodex.ts`.

Asambodo arụnyere maka **mmepe localhost** . Maka mbugharị ndị dịpụrụ adịpụ, debanye aha nke gị na njikwa onye nrụpụta nke ọ bụla.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                          | Onye na-enye                  | Ihe ndetu                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Koodu Claude (Anthropic)      | Ọha ahịa — ọ dịghị ihe nzuzo chọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | koodu Claude                  | Wepu redirect URI. Ihe ndabara: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI                | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)               | Chọrọ ndakọrịta `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)               | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi coding (Moonshot)        | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Koodu Muse (Meta)             | Mweghara nhọrọ maka ọha Muse CLI ngwaọrụ-asọpụta onye ahịa id. Hapụ ahazighị ka iji onye ahịa ọhaneze esiri esi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)          | Chọrọ ndakọrịta `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)          | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot                | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot                   | Mweghara nhọrọ maka GitHub Enterprise Copilot's OAuth id ahịa. Daghachi na ndabara ọhaneze `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` mgbe edobereghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot                | Mwechapụ nhọrọ maka GitHub Copilot ndị ahịa mwekota ID ezigara na `Copilot-Integration-Id` na `Editor-Plugin-Version` nkụnye eji isi mee. Ihe ndabara na `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Nhọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Debanye aha ngwa na `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` na redirect URI `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` na scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Daghachi azụ na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`            | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | OAuth nzuzo nzuzo maka GitLab Duo. Nhọrọ — PKCE eruba anaghị achọ ihe nzuzo. Daghachi azụ na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`                       | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Wepu GitLab base URL ( GitLab ji aka ya kwado). Ihe ndabara na `https://gitlab.com`. Daghachi azụ na `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. A na-eji ya mgbe ụdị `_DUO_` adịghị edozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` riri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENCY`                 | Qoder                         | Nke kacha na-agba ọsọ n'otu oge qodercli (bipụta spawns ma ọ bụ ACP kpaliri); mmezi arịrịọ kwụ n'ahịrị FIFO. Ewepụghị (ndapụta) = enweghị okpu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_CLI_QUEUE_TIMEOUT_MS`                | Qoder                         | Ogologo oge ole ka arịrịọ Qoder na-echere oghere qodercli (naanị na-eji okpu setịpụ) tupu ọ daa na 429 `qodercli_busy` + `Retry-After`. `120000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `QODER_CLI_TRANSPORT`                       | Qoder                         | `print` (ndabere) spawns `qodercli --print` kwa arịrịọ; `acp` na-edobe otu usoro `qodercli --acp` na-adịgide adịgide kwa PAT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | Qoder                         | njem ACP : gbuo usoro qodercli na-adịgide adịgide mgbe oge a na-adịghị arụ ọrụ gasịrị. `600000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`          | Qoder                         | njem ACP : njedebe maka qodercli ịza ACP `initialize`. `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                         | njem ACP : megharịa usoro qodercli na-adịgide adịgide mgbe ọtụtụ nnọkọ a gachara. `500`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Weebụ Blackbox                | N'ihu `tk` akara izipu ka `validated` na `/api/chat`. Achọrọ mgbe Blackbox na-amanye ndakọrịta token; ma ọ bụghị ya, OmniRoute na-adaghachi na UUID na-enweghị usoro. Lee mbipụta #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Vision Bridge nche okporo ụzọ | OpenAI-dakọtara base URL maka oku ọhụụ-akwa na-abụghị Anthropic. Edebere na ihe nketa OpenAI URL env ma ọ bụ api.openai.com. Tụtụ aka na OmniRoute's `/v1` self-loop ma ọ bụ ihe ọ bụla OpenAI-compat endpoint (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). mbipụta #2232. Mgbe URL bụ OmniRoute nke onwe `/v1`, sub-arịrịọ nkọwa na-eziga `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` wee gosipụta ya site na nzere nbibi nke onwe (`sk_omniroute` sentinel na ọnọdụ mpaghara, ma ọ bụ `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) yabụ `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ntinye ọrụ na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Vision Bridge nche okporo ụzọ | igodo API maka URL dị n'elu. Na-ewepụ onye na-eweta OpenAI / Google env vars maka oku ọhụụ-àkwà mmiri na-abụghị nke Anthropic. ụdị Anthropic na-edobe ụzọ igodo Anthropic raara onwe ha nye. mbipụta #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Vision Bridge nche okporo ụzọ | Ogologo oge ole ka a na-echekwa nsonaazụ ntụgharị "enweghị onye ga-eji eme ihe, na ms (ndabere `30000`). Ụkpụrụ ezighi ezi ma ọ bụ nke na-adịghị mma na-adaghachi na ndabara; `0` gbanyụọ cache adịghị mma. Isi mmalite: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_IMAGE_NORMALIZATION`             | Ọhụụ onyonyo normalization    | Wepụ (wepụ = anya, mgbanwe omume efu; `1`/`true`/`on` na-enyere aka). Normalizes ọ bụla inline (base64 data URI / Claude `source.data`) oyiyi na nkata arịrịọ ozu tupu izipu: EXIF nghazi etinyere, RGB (alfa flattened na-acha ọcha), ogologo onu `<= 4096` px, ngụkọta pikselụ `<= ~4M`; A na-edogharị onyonyo ndị chọrọ mgbanwe dị ka ntọala JPEG ( PNGs na-enweghị alfa na-anọkwa na-enweghị atụ PNG), RGB JPEG/PNG na-akwadorịrị na-agafe na-emetụghị aka. A na-edozi mmefu ego. Ezubere pipeline ọhụụ Qwen2-VL-style nke na-ajụ onyonyo oversized/exotic nwere mperi parse 400 ma ọ bụ kpọgidere ihe efu. A naghị ebudata URLs http (s) dịpụrụ adịpụ; ọnụọgụ abụọ `sharp` na-efu ma ọ bụ onyonyo a na-apụghị ịkọwapụta na-agafe emetụghị aka (ọdịda-emeghe). Ịhazigharị nnukwu onyonyo na-agbakwunye latency arịrịọ. Isi mmalite: `open-sse/utils/visionImageNormalization.ts`. |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Gaa na [nzere Google Cloud Console →](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Mepụta OAuth 2.0 Client ID (ụdị: "ngwa Weebụ")
> 3. Tinye ihe nkesa gị URL dị ka ikike redirect URI
> 4. Dochie ụkpụrụ nzere na `.env`.

## 12. Onye na-enye ọrụ-onye nnọchi anya na-agbagha

Wepụ nkụnye eji isi mee `User-Agent` ezigara onye ọ bụla na-eweta elu. A na-edozi nke a n'ike n'ike n'oge ọ na-agba ọsọ site na klaasị ndị mmebe:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Uru ndabara | Mgbe imelite |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Mgbe Anthropic wepụtara ụdị CLI ọhụrụ |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Tọọ na `1`/`true` ka ibugharịa aha ngwa ngwa ngwa ndị ọzọ na Anthropic n'ụzọ abụọ metụtara Anthropic (nwaafọ OAuth na CLIProxyAPI). Site na ndabara, onye mmebe ahụ na-akpọ aha ndị na-abụghị Claude-Code ngwá ọrụ (Claude Code canonical mapping ebe otu dị, ma ọ bụghị PascalCase) wee tụgharịa ha na nzaghachi site na `_toolNameMap`, yabụ, a naghị ajụ harnesses na snake_case ngwaọrụ dị ka akara mkpịsị aka ndị ahịa ndị ọzọ. Na-emezigharị naanị. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Mgbe OpenAI na-emelite Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Wepụ ụdị onye ahịa Codex na-adabereghị na eriri UA zuru ezu |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Wepụ `x-grok-client-version` maka ntinye, mmekọrịta nlereanya, na koodu OAuth ngwaọrụ, gbakwunyere Grok CLI Onye ọrụ-Nnọchite anya maka ntinye na mmekọrịta ụdị. Nabata SemVer na nhọrọ prerelease/build metadata, ruo mkpụrụedemede 128 ka ewepụchara oghere ọcha. Ụkpụrụ atọghị, nke efu, adịghị mma, ma ọ bụ akara nwere nkwụsịtụ na-eji ntụtụ ebugoro. Ntinye njupụta nke onye ọrụ-onye nnọchite anya na-ejigide ụzọ. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Wepụ ụdị koodu Claude akpọsara na-adabereghị na `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic ọnụ ụzọ ụfọdụ ụdị na uru a (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Wepụ suffix agwa 3 OmniRoute gbakwunyere na `cc_version=` na ngọngọ ịgba ụgwọ Claude. Bump n'akụkụ `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — itinye naanị ụdị ahụ na-akpọsa ụzọ `version.revision` enweghị ezigbo ọnụọgụ ọnụọgụ abụọ. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Wepụ ụdị Copilot CLI akpọsara na-adabereghị na `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Mgbe GitHub Copilot Chat na-emelite |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CLI_CL` | _(Edobeghi)_ | Wepụ ndepụta mgbanwe piper Antigravity CLI `agy`) nke akọọrọ dị ka `cl=` na CLI Onye nnọchite anya. Ọ bụghị ewepụtara na mbipute ma ọ bụ kpughere ọha na-egosi; mkpụrụ n'otu ụdị sitere na njide dị ndụ (1.4.4 → `1003595037`). Etughari na ụdị amabeghị na-ewepụ `cl=` kama ịkpọsa ndepụta mgbanwe ihe nrụpụta ọzọ. |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Mgbe Kiro IDE na-emelite |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Wepụ Kiro social device-koodu `clientId` (ID ọha) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Nbanye: ozi CRC zuru oke n'otu-frame na iyi mmemme Kiro (mezie iyi ndị mebiri emebi) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD kwa Kiro kredit; na-atụgharị `meteringEvent` kredit ka ọ bụrụ ọnụ ahịa arịrịọ ọ bụla |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Daa nzaghachi Kiro nke na-akọ enweghị kredit meter kama ịdekọ ya enweghị ụgwọ |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Mgbe Qoder CLI na-emelite |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Mgbe Cursor emelitere |

> [!TIP]
> Ị nwere ike ịgbakwunye onye ọrụ-onye nnọchi anya gabigara maka** nke ọ bụla** onye na-eweta na-eji ụkpụrụ `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Onye mmebe ahụ na-ewuli aha env var nke ọma.

## 13. CLI ndakọrịta mkpịsị aka mkpịsị aka

Mgbe enyere ya aka, OmniRoute na-atụgharị ihe nkụnye eji isi HTTP na mpaghara ahụ JSON ka ọ dabara na mbinye aka nke ngwa CLI gọọmentị. Nke a na-ebelata ohere nke ịdekọ akaụntụ mgbe ị na-echekwa IP proxy gị.

**Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Onye na-eweta ya

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe       | Nkwalite | Mmetụta                          |
| ------------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Mimics Codex CLI mbinye aka      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Mimics Claude Koodu mbinye aka   |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Mimics GitHub Copilot mbinye aka |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Mimics Antigravity mbinye aka    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Mimics Cursor mbinye aka         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Mimics Kimi Ntinye mbinye aka    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | mbinye aka koodu Mimics Kilo     |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Mimics Cline mbinye aka          |

### Gburugburu ụwa

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Nkwalite | Mmetụta                                                              |
| ------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL`   | `=1`     | Kwado ndakọrịta akara mkpịsị aka maka** niile** ndị na-enye otu oge. |

### Kimi Ndokwa CLI njirimara na-ewepụ

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe      | Ọdabara            | Isi mmalite faịlụ                        | Nkọwa                                                   |
| ----------------------- | ------------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`           | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Wepụ ụdị Kimi CLI ezitere n'oge oku OAuth/API.          |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(dabara ejidere)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Wepu ngwaọrụ Kimi eweghara ID ejiri na nkụnye ndị ahịa. |

> [!NOTE]
> Njirimara a na-arụ ọrụ n'akụkụ ihe nbibi nke onye ọrụ-onye ọrụ (§12). Sistemụ akara mkpisiaka na-ejikwa ịtụ nkụnye eji isi mee yana ịtụ mpaghara ahụ, ebe onye nnọchi anya onye ọrụ na-ejikwa eriri UA akọwapụtara. Enwere ike ịgbanye ha abụọ n'onwe ha.
