| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oke ọdịda na-esochi onwe ha maka ndị na-enye ọrụ OAuth tupu ihe mgbochi amalite ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oge windo mweghachi (ms) maka ihe mgbochi onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oke ọdịda na-esochi onwe ha maka ndị na-enye ọrụ API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oge windo mweghachi (ms) maka ihe mgbochi onye na-enye ọrụ API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oke ọdịda na-esochi onwe ha maka ndị na-enye ọrụ dị n'ime mpaghara (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oge windo mweghachi (ms) maka ihe mgbochi onye na-enye ọrụ dị n'ime mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: ọdịda ole a na-anabata n'ime windo tupu onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth niile abanye n'oge jụrụ oyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: windo (ms) na-agagharị maka ịgụ ọdịda nke ndị na-enye ọrụ OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: oge jụrụ oyi (ms) mgbe oke onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth ruru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọtụtụ mmụba kachasị elu nke onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth na-eji maka ịrị elu resetTimeout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth na-ebuli ọkwa mgbe usoro mmeghe ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: ọdịda ole a na-anabata n'ime windo tupu onye na-enye ọrụ API-key niile abanye n'oge jụrụ oyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: windo (ms) na-agagharị maka ịgụ ọdịda nke ndị na-enye ọrụ API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: oge jụrụ oyi (ms) mgbe oke onye na-enye ọrụ API-key ruru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-enye ọrụ API-key na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọtụtụ mmụba kachasị elu nke onye na-enye ọrụ API-key na-eji maka ịrị elu resetTimeout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-enye ọrụ API-key na-ebuli ọkwa mgbe usoro mmeghe ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta ọrụ: ọnụọgụ ọdịda tupu onye na-eweta ọrụ mpaghara dum abanye n'oge nkwụsịtụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta ọrụ: windo na-atụgharị maka ịgụ ọdịda (ms) maka ndị na-eweta ọrụ mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta ọrụ: oge nkwụsịtụ (ms) ozugbo onye na-eweta ọrụ mpaghara ruru oke ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                 | Ntugharị igodo ọ bụla (ms) etinyere site na ntụgharị igodo ọtụtụ igodo API mgbe 429 elu na-ebughị `Retry-After` (#14573). Ụkpụrụ na-abụghị nke ziri ezi ma ọ bụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_AVALANCHE_PROTECTION_ENABLED`                      | `false`   | `open-sse/services/accountFallback/antiAvalanche.ts` | Nchedo mgbochi avalanche nke a na-ahọrọ iji rụọ ọrụ: naanị `true` akọwapụtara nke ọma na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ. Mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ, 429 nke kpọrọ oke tokens kwa nkeji (yana 400 nke context-overflow) na-akwụsị failover na-agbasawanye n'ofe igodo ndị fọdụrụ nke onye na-eweta ọrụ. 429 nke quota gwụchara na-aga n'ihu na failover n'agbanyeghị etu prompt ahụ hà, ebe HTTP 413 anaghị emetụta (ọ na-aga n'ihu na omume retry-on-another-account nke #13350). |
| `OMNIROUTE_AVALANCHE_BURST_COOLDOWN_MS`                       | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/accountFallback/antiAvalanche.ts` | Model-mkpọchi cooldown (ms) etinyere mgbe TPM 429 nche ọkụ, kama mkpọchi na-arị elu. Ntọgharị elu dị ogologo na-emeri mgbe niile. Ụkpụrụ na-abụghị nke ziri ezi ma ọ bụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Ọkwa backoff nke a na-ewere onye na-eweta ọrụ nke pin cache-context dị ka onye na-arịa ọrịa na-adịgide adịgide, wee tufuo pin ahụ maka failover.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Windo mgbochi mgbanwe ugboro ugboro (ms) nke na-anabata oge nkwụsịtụ dị mkpirikpi tupu atụfu pin cache-context.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                                  | Nhazi                                                    |
| -------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ịmepụta koodu na-ewe ogologo oge**   | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (nkeji 15)                   |
| **Ịkekọrịta oke ogologo ndụ iyi**      | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (nkeji 21)            |
| **Ịkwụsị ngwa ngwa maka API mmepụta**  | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                      |
| **Modelụ na-eche echiche ogologo oge** | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (nkeji 5 n'etiti chunks) |

## 16. Ndekọ

Sistemụ ndekọ na-ede ma na stdout ma na faịlụ ndekọ ndị a na-atụgharịgharị. A na-agụ nhazi niile site na `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Mgbanwe                                   | Ndabara                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Ọkwa ndekọ kacha nta: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Ọdịdị mmepụta: `text` (nke mmadụ nwere ike ịgụ) ma ọ bụ `json` (ahaziri ahazi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Dee ndekọ na faịlụ yana stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Ụzọ faịlụ ndekọ (n'ihe metụtara mgbọrọgwụ prọjektị ma ọ bụ `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Ogo faịlụ kachasị tupu ntụgharị. Ọ na-anabata: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ma ọ bụ bytes nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe faịlụ ndekọ ngwa ndị a tụgharịworo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Ọnụọgụ ndabere faịlụ ndekọ ndị a tụgharịworo kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ/oku na ebe nchekwa data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Ọnụọgụ ndenye ndekọ oku kachasị na buffer dị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `call_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(edoghị ya)_              | Manye ndekọ arịrịọ zuru ezu ka ọ gbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ, na-akagbu ntọala dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (1 elekere)      | Ogologo oge kachasị maka ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ nọ n'ọrụ ma bụrụ nke a hapụrụ tupu nhicha n'ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MAX_PENDING_DETAILS`                     | `5000`                     | Nkọwa kachasị na-echere (integer karịrị efu); oke ọnụ ọgụgụ: mgbe a na-ehichapụ; oke byte/map: mgbe a na-etinye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Chekwaa iberibe stream na pipeline artifacts mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Nhọrọ nke onye ọrụ ga-eme (`true`) — agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji chekwaa ohere diski.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Ogo artifact ndekọ oku pipeline kachasị na KB mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `proxy_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Tinye IP ndị ahịa/egress na prefix akaụntụ na ndekọ console `[ProxyEgress]`. Ihe ndekọ proxy-log nke dashboard/ebe nchekwa data na-edowe nkọwa niile.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji dekọọ `first_chunk_ms` (site na mbido izipu ruo na byte mbụ bara uru nke body) n'ahịrị proxy-log. Ọ na-etinye body upstream ọ bụla n'ime stream passthrough umengwụ ma na-eme patch n'ahịrị ahụ ozugbo byte ahụ rutere; agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara ka response gafee n'enweghị mgbanwe. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 nkeji)          | Ugboro ole `src/lib/logRotation.ts` na-enyochagharị nha faịlụ ndekọ nọ n'ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Ogologo string kachasị a na-edowe na chat log artifacts (ndabara bụ 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Ọnụọgụ ihe array a na-edowe site na ngwụcha mgbe a na-ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Omimi ntinye kachasị tupu ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Object keys kachasị a na-edowe na chat log payloads (0 = enweghị oke).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Nha body arịrịọ/response dum (KB) tupu e jiri nchịkọta nkịtị dochie ya kama clone zuru ezu. Welie nke a ma ọ bụrụ na mkparịta ụka agentic ogologo na-egosi placeholder kama ozi ndị ahụ n'ezie na dashboard.                                                                                                                     |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Mgbe ọ bụ true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` na-amafere mbipụ dabere na nha. Maka debug naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## 17. Nchekwa nchekwa

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                           | Ọdabara                                                                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEAP_DIAGNOSTICS_ENABLED`         | `0` (gbanyụọ)                                                                              | Tọọ `1` ka ịdebanye metric nrụgide V8 site na `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`. Nha ndekọ na ọnụọgụ ụgbọ elu, arịọla ọdịnaya, traktị ma ọ bụ mkpọ foto. Na-agba ọsọ n'ime ihe ndekọ-loop stall recorder, yabụ `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS=0` na-agbanyụkwa ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEAP_DIAGNOSTICS_THRESHOLD_MB`    | `900`                                                                                      | Ọnụ ụzọ dị mma maka heapUsed (MiB) nke na-ebute ndekọ Nwepu nyocha. Ụkpụrụ ezighi ezi na-eji 900 MiB; A na-ejedebe ozi dị elu otu ugboro n'ime sekọnd 60.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`                        | _auto_ (ọla efu);**`1024` na foto Docker**                                                 | **Akwadoro** Docker/standalone V8 oke oke (MB). Mgbe edobereghị ya, a na-ahazi ya nke ọma (~ 35% nke sistemu RAM, kpọgidere na `[512, 4096]`); `512` bụ naanị ala mgbe enweghị ike ịgụta ebe nchekwa mkpokọta. Na `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), an** doro anya** agbakwunyere uru dị ka `--max-old-space-size` na** merie** n'elu ọkọlọtọ obo NODE_OPTIONS na-emegiderịta (ọkọlọtọ ikpeazụ V8). `omniroute serve` ka na-ahọrọ ikpo NODE_OPTIONS dị adị (#5238). Edokwala ma nọmba dị iche iche — usoro a na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị aha ma ụkpụrụ na onye mmeri.** Onyonyo Docker gọọmentị na-edobe `1024` mgbe niile, yabụ ndozigharị anaghị aga ebe ahụ.** Onye nnọchite koodu `/v1/responses` chọrọ `8192`–`12288` gbakwunyere cgroup headroom — hụ [Docker Ntuziaka — oge ojiri gaa RAM](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`                  | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; gbanyụọ mgbe emebere `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB` ) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Usoro zuru oke-RSS fuse (MB) maka ebe nchekwa native/external nke V8 `heapUsed` enweghị ike ịhụ; n'elu ya arịrịọ ọhụrụ nweta 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS adịkarịghị ada ma ọ gachara, yabụ jikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                      | `50`                                                                                       | Ndenye sistemu echekwara kacha elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                           | Nha nnukwu cache ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`                        | `300000` (minti 5)                                                                         | Ngwa cache ntinye TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                    | `100`                                                                                      | Okpomọkụ kacha echekwara = 0 nzaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                   | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                           | Ngụ cache kacha elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`                      | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                         | Ntinye cache semantic TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                         | nkeji SQLite kwachaa. `0` gbanyụọ mmalite na ọsọ oge; ụkpụrụ ndị ọzọ dị n'okpuru `60000` daa azụ na ndabara. Uru ndị dị n'elu `2147483647` ms jigidere ngụ oge Node kachasị, na-ezere njupụta na ntuli aka 1ms. Mbụ na-agba ọsọ mgbe 1s; ndị na-agụ oge anaghị emegharị ma na-agba ọsọ anaghị agafe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_BATCH_SIZE`  | `2000`                                                                                     | Ahịrị kwa SQLite prune DELETE, a kpọgidere ya na `[100, 50000]`; na-enye aka aka aka omume n'etiti batches.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_MAX_BATCHES` | `50`                                                                                       | Ekekọrịtara mmefu ego ogbe DELETE kwa ọsọ gafere ngafe okpu nke nhọrọ, agbakwunyere na `[1, 10000]`. Ndekọ azụ na-aga n'ihu na ọsọ ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_ROWS`          | `0`                                                                                        | Okpu ahiri SQLite nhọrọ (`0` = ọ dịghị). Mgbe ihichapụ `expires_at <=` ahụ run-start ISO timestamp, chụpụ `created_at` kacha ochie, wee `rowid`, gụnyere ahịrị ndị dị ndụ, dabere na mmefu ego nkekọrịta. A na-agbaji ọnụ ọgụgụ ka ọ bụrụ ọnụọgụ (ruo oke ọnụọgụ nchekwa nchekwa). Ihe efu, nke na-abụghị ọnụọgụ, na-enweghị njedebe ma ọ bụ nke na-adịghị mma maka mgbanwe anọ nke prune na-adaghachi na ndabara ha; irighiri ihe gbadaa ala. Ikwachaa anaghị agbanwe TTL, ebe nchekwa LRU ma ọ bụ vector-cache omume.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                | `5`                                                                                        | Mgbọrọgwụ nke mpaghara-corpus nke na-edobe ihe ndekọ ebe nchekwa n'otu oge (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: na njedebe a na-achụpụ ma wughachi ya na ajụjụ na-esote ya. Ejidebere na opekempe nke `1`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`                         | `50`                                                                                       | Ihe omume iyi kacha nso nso a na nchekwa ihe nlele ndụ Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                                                                               | Ihe ngbanwe gburugburu ụwa-ogologo gabigara (token) maka ndị na-eweta ọ bụla na-enweghị mgbanwe `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Mgbe atọrọ ya** dochie** katalọgụ, dashboard gabigara na ụkpụrụ ndekọ aha maka ndị na-eweta ya; mgbe a tọghị ya, OmniRoute na-eji yinye arụnyere arụnyere ya, ebe ikpeazụ ya bụ `128000`. Hụ [ogologo okirikiri nke onye na-eweta ya](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`                         | `100`                                                                                      | Edobere nnukwu isi ụlọ akara ngosi mgbe ị na-enyocha oke ojiji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Ogologo okirikiri nke onye na-eweta ya (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) na-agụkwa a** onye na-eweta ya** ụdị nke ewuru aha ya site n'aka onye na-eweta ID n'oge ojiri gaa, yabụ enweghị ike depụta ya n'usoro n'usoro na tebụl dị n'elu ma ọ bụ na `.env.example`.

**Aha.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, ebe `<PROVIDER>` bụ onye na-eweta ID elu-cased, na agwa ọ bụla na-abụghị `A-Z` / `0-9` dochie `_`. Onye na-eweta ID bụ nke a na-ahụ anya na mkpebi utu aha (akụkụ `provider` nke eriri `provider/model` ozugbo edozichara utu aha dị mkpirikpi). Omenala OpenAI- ma ọ bụ Anthropic-dakọtara ọnụ na-eji ọnụ ha emepụtara ID, yabụ UUID bụ akụkụ nke aha agbanwe agbanwe. Uru bụ ọnụọgụ ọnụọgụ dị mma nke token; ihe ọ bụla ọzọ (efu, `0`, adịghị mma, na-abụghị ọnụọgụ) na-eleghara anya na mkpebi na-aga n'ihu na nzọụkwụ ọzọ.

| Onye na-enye ID                                             | Aha mgbanwe                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| agent router                                                | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| oghere oghere                                               | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Ibu ụzọ.** Maka otu ụzọ `provider/model`, nzọụkwụ mbụ na-eweta uru na-emeri:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` maka onye na-eweta ya.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (mmebi gburugburu ụwa).
3. Window ihe nlere ọ bụla: ihe na-adịgide adịgide gabigara (nke edobere na dashboard, ma ọ bụ tinye ya na akpaghị aka site na nchọpụta nchọpụta, lee `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), ma ọ bụghị ya, katalọgụ / ụdị.dev synced synced.
4. Ndebanye aha onye na-eweta `defaultContextLength`.
5. Ụdị aha heuristics (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` / `codex` 400000).
6. Mmebi nke onye na-eweta ihe arụnyere, wee `128000`.

Usoro abụọ a metụtara gburugburu ebe obibi** onye na-eweta ya niile** : ụdị ọ bụla nke onye na-eweta ahụ na-enweta otu oke ahụ, ha na-emeri kwa dashboard nke ụdị ọ bụla. Mgbe naanị otu ụdị nke onye na-eweta chọrọ windo dị iche, jiri ihe nfefe nke otu ụdị na dashboard kama.

**Oke.** Agbụ a na-eme ka nlele oge arịrịọ-ohere ọnọdụ (njụjụ `context_length_exceeded` / "Ntinye karịrị windo ọnọdụ" jụrụ na `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), mmefu ego mkpakọ ngwa ngwa yana oke mkpokọta mkpokọta. `context_length` nke `GET /v1/models` kpọsara ka nwere ike si na katalọgụ ma ọ bụ metadata ndekọ ma ekwekwaghị n'ezie na ọ ga-egosipụta na ọ ga-emebi gburugburu.

**Ọmụmaatụ.** Onye na-eweta ihe nlereanya ya na-anabata windo token 1,050,000 ebe katalọgụ na-akọ 128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Malitegharịa ihe nkesa ahụ mgbe ị gbanwee ya: a na-agụ uru ahụ site na gburugburu usoro ihe nkesa. Na** ngwa desktọọpụ** ihe nkesa na-agba ọsọ dị ka usoro nwatakịrị ma ọ naghị ahụ usoro agbanwe agbanwe agbanwe mgbe emechara ngwa ahụ; tinye ahịrị n'ime faịlụ `.env` ngwa ngwa (usoro nyocha na [Electron Guide: Environment file searchup](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup) ), wee kwụsị kpamkpam wee malitegharịa ngwa ahụ.

### Mkpakọ

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara | Nkọwa                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | ịtọhapụ | Tụkwasị oru ngo `.rtk/filters.json` na-enweghị a `.rtk/trust.json` hash. Jiri naanị na mmepe mpaghara a na-achịkwa. |

### Igwe ebe nchekwa (atụmatụ 21)

### Ọnụ ihe omume-loop nke ebe nchekwa, nka na ume ọhụrụ (#10349)

OmniRoute bu a** otu Node usoro** . Ebe nchekwa extraction/retrieval, nkà ịgba ntụtụ, na onye na-eweta ihe ngosi na-agba ọsọ na nke ahụ** otu ihe omume akaghị** dị ka `GET /healthz` na dashboard. Ha abụghị eriri onye ọrụ.

| Ọrụ                           | Koodu                              | Ọdabara                                      | Njikwa onye ọrụ                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Mwepụta/iweghachi ebe nchekwa | `src/lib/memory/`                  | Dashboard**memoryEnabled** (ndabere na)      | Gbanyụọ** Ntọala → Ebe nchekwa** . Enweghị mgba ọkụ env kill dị iche karịa ịkwụsị njirimara na ntọala.                 |
| Ngwunye nka                   | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | Dashboard**skillsEnabled** (ndabere na)      | Gbanyụọ** Ntọala → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Mkpọtụ igbe igbe dị n'okpuru naanị ngwunye mgbe agbanyere ntụtụ. |
| Token nweta ume               | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | Gbanyere maka ndị na-eweta OAuth/web ejikọrọ | Kwụpụ onye na-eweta ya ma ọ bụ hapụ akara ka ọ dị irè; o nweghi `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` env today.                           |

Ọ bụrụ na `/healthz` adịghị nwayọ na igbe dị jụụ, gbanyụọ ikike + ebe nchekwa na mbụ, wee lelee ibu catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Atụmatụ ndị a na-ewepụta na `await` point mana ka na-asọ mpi maka otu eri.

Na-etinye akwa oyi akwa, ụlọ ahịa vector na mkpisigharị maka sistemụ nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                   | Ọdabara                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (minti 5)          | TTL maka cache ntinye ebe nchekwa (kwa mbinye source/model/dim ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Edebere ndenye kacha LRU n'ime oghere ntinye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id maka nbanye `@huggingface/transformers` mpaghara MiniLM pipeline (~ 23 MB int8, ~ 400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id maka tebụl nchọta potion/Model2Vec static. Ebudata ya dị umengwụ n'ime cache dir.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | A na-eji akwụkwọ ndekọ aha na-echekwa faịlụ ụdị potion static. Ihe ndabara n'okpuru `DATA_DIR` mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Wepu mmakọ Ihu Hub base URL nke `staticPotion.ts` na-eji (dịka ọmụmaatụ njedebe enyo maka ntọlite ikuku nwere oghere).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Top-K nke nchọta `sqlite-vec` brute-force vector na-eji n'ime `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Ntugharị ọkwa Fusion mgbe niile `k` maka ngwakọ FTS5 + vector eweghachite (ntụziaka sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Test/diagnostic seam na `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): mgbe `true`, na-amanye ụlọ ahịa vector ka ọ `null` (na-eme ka gburugburu cloud/WASM na-enweghị `sqlite-vec`), iweghachite ebe nchekwa na-eweda ya na nchọ okwu FTS5. Hapụ enweghị ntọala na mmepụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                | igodo API maka azụ azụ Notion (nke `genericBackend.ts` mara azụ azụ azụ na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Ndabere URL maka Notion API (nwere ike ịkagbu maka ndị ọzọ Notion kwadoro onwe ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(Edobeghi)_                | igodo API maka Obsidian Vault backend (nke `genericBackend.ts` mara azụ azụ azụ na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Base URL maka Obsidian Vault API (nwere ike ịkwụsị maka oghere dịpụrụ adịpụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | TV6 pịnyere ebe nchekwa decay master mgba ọkụ.** Nwepu (nke mbụ)** — mkpochapụ** na-ehichapụ** ncheta rere ure. Gbanyụọ ya, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bụ telemetry dị ọcha na ọ nweghị ihe ehichapụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (ụbọchị) mgbe nke ahụ gasịrị, ebe nchekwa `episodic` na-ejighị ya na-emebi. `0` na-emekwa ka mgbochi nke episodic kwa. Ụdị na-adịgide adịgide (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) na-adịghị egbochi mgbe niile. Elekere ire ere na-adaberekwa na `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ebe nchekwa gbara `>=` nke a ọtụtụ oge na-agaghị emebi emebi n'agbanyeghị ụdị. `0` na-ewepụ mgbochi ohere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (nkwarụ)                | Ogologo oge (sekọnd) maka ịsachapụ ire ere oge oge na `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/unset = enweghị mkpochapụ oge. Nbanye ugboro abụọ: chọkwara `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(Edobeghi)_                | Id ndị na-eweta rịkọm nke kewapụrụ (ọnọdụ enweghị mmetụta) na-anabata ozi `system`** naanị na index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Maka ndị a, oghere nchekwa nchekwa etiti dị n'etiti adịghị mma na mkparịta ụka ọtụtụ ntụgharị, yabụ ebe nchekwa bụ merged/prepended dị ka ozi sistemụ na-eduga kama. Ihe ndabara na naanị `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo`; gbatịkwuo maka oghere njedebe dakọtara na OpenAI nke kwadoro onwe ya (dịka Qwen3.5 +/3.6) nke ndebiri nkata ya na-akwado otu mmachi ozi-usoro na-eduzi otu. |

### Obere-RAM Docker Ihe atụ

`128` bụ naanị dashboard. Ndị na-etinye koodu na `FATAL ERROR` a n'oge ogologo `/v1/responses`. Ejila ihe atụ a dịka ọnụ ụzọ Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Ụdị prefix ejiri maka ids nlereanya na `GET /v1/models`. `dual` na-akpọsa ma mkpirisi utu aha dị mkpụmkpụ yana prefix nke ndị na-eweta canonical maka ụdị ọ bụla (ndakọrịta azụ — na-eme ka katalọgụ ahụ okpukpu abụọ); `alias` na-ewepụta otu id kwa ụdị; `canonical` na-ewepụta naanị prefix onye na-eweta-id (ndị na-eweta aha ha bụbu id nke canonical na-edobe otu ntinye ha). Ndị ahịa nwere ike ịkagbu otu arịrịọ site na `?prefix=alias`. Lee [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |

## PromptQL Onye na-enye ebe egwuregwu (Unofficial/Experimental)

Ntụgharị azụ azụ GraphQL session bridge for quick.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Ihe ndabara — niile nhọrọ na-atụ aka na njedebe ebe egwuregwu ọha; kagbuo naanị maka ntinye self-hosted/alternate PromptQL.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe           | Ọdabara                                                            | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                       | Nkọwa                                                       |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | ebe njedebe GraphQL ejiri maka ọrụ chat/session.            |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | ebe ngwụcha GraphQL na-ajụ kredit balance/usage.            |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | A na-eji njedebe njedebe maka ume ọhụrụ token kacha mma.    |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Oge kacha (ms) iji mee ntuli `thread_events` tupu oge agwụ. |

## Oke koodu ojiji kilo

Ntụle USD nkeonwe yana nyocha ojiji Kilo Pass maka onye na-eweta koodu Kilo. Nhọrọ — ihe ndabara na Kilo API ọha; mebie naanị maka nrụnye relay/test. Nyocha na-eji akara nnweta OAuth dị na njikọ ahụ.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Ọdabara               | Isi mmalite faịlụ                     | Nkọwa                                                                 |
| ------------------ | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL`     | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | Base URL ejiri nweta nguzozi koodu Kilo nkeonwe yana ojiji Kilo Pass. |

## Adobe Firefly Web Provider (Unofficial/Experimental)

Oge nchọgharị na-akwalite ume ọhụrụ maka ndị na-eweta webụsaịtị Adobe Firefly (`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Nhọrọ — niile nke ndabara ka edobere maka ịwụnye desktọpụ nkịtị.

> **Ewepụrụ na #9255.** Oge ọsọ ọsọ Chrome nke CDP jikọtara (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) jiri ọrụ nbanye Playwright dochie ya, na mkpịsị aka ya adịkwaghị. ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART variables, gbakwunyere ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS na ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ka a na-agụghị ebe ọ bụla na codebase — mwube ha enweghị mmetụta.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                   | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------ | ------------ | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`          | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Tọọ `1` maka ezigbo Chrome enweghị isi (nke amaara maka imepụta; naanị nbipu).                          |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`          | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ihe nchọgharị na-arụ ọrụ na-ekpo ọkụ opt-in/out. `0` na-ewepụ ọkụ ọkụ (mgbake etiti 408 ka na-emetụta). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`          | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ịnọgide na-enwe oge Adobe Firefly na diski.                                         |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(Edobeghi)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Opekempe (ms) amanye n'etiti ntinye aka na-esochi, na-ewepụ ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya.                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(Edobeghi)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere ọzọ (ms) agbakwunyere ka ọ nwesịrị ihe ịga nke ọma, na-ewepụ ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya.          |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(Edobeghi)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Oge nkwụsị (ms) tupu ịnyefe arịrịọ ọgbọ, na-ewepụ ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya.                            |

## 19. Mmekọrịta Model (Dev)

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                  | Ọdabara       | Isi mmalite faịlụ                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(Edobeghi)_  | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Mfefe siri ike maka ụdịrị mịkọrị ọnụ ahịa dev. Tọọ ntọala = nsọpụrụ > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no`** na-emeri DB** ma na-awụpụ ma mmekọrịta oge oge yana nyocha `getModelsDevPricing()` SQL/JSON (nweghachi mgbe etinyere dashboard n'otu akaghị ihe omume). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` Force mekọrịta na. Ọnụ ego save/clear ka na-akpọ `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, nke na-abụghị op n'okpuru nkeji 60 ma ọ bụ `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Ogologo oge mmekọrịta katalọgụ ụdị-oge mmepe n'ime sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Ogologo oge (sekọnd) maka nhazigharị nke onwe-window reconciler (5004): pins onye na-eweta-kwupụtara windo site na nchọpụta `/models` ka `auto:discovery` na-agafe mgbe ha si na katalọgụ ahụ pụọ. Tọọ na `0` iji gbanyụọ. Na-ejigharị data emekọrịtalarị (ọ nweghị ihe ọhụrụ ewepụtara); anaghị edegharị `manual` gabiga.                                                                                                                                       |
