## 20. Ntọala kpọmkwem onye na-eweta

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                             | Ọdabara                                | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_CATALOG_TTL_MS`                    | `86400000` (24h)                       | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterCatalog.ts`                                             | OpenRouter ụdị katalọgụ cache TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MODEL_CATALOG_INCLUDE_NAMES`                  | `true`                                 | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                   | Tinye mpaghara `name` omume ngosi na nzaghachi `/v1/models`. Gbanyụọ maka ndị ahịa na-atụ anya IDs naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_GEMINI_WEB_ARGON_ENABLED`           | `0` (gbanyụọ)                          | `open-sse/config/providers/registry/gemini/web/index.ts`                           | Ịbanye (#16573). Tọọ `1` (ma ọ bụ `true`/`yes`) iji kpọsaa `gweb/gemini-4-argon` na webụ Gemini wee hapụ onye ọrụ ya họrọ ọnọdụ ahụ. Gbanyụọ na ndabara n'ihi na enyochabeghị ndị ahọpụtara ụdị menu Argon megide akaụntụ Weebụ Gemini dị ndụ; mgbe gbanyụọ, ihe nlereanya na-adịghị depụtara na a na-ajụ arịrịọ maka ya dị ka ihe na-amaghị ihe nlereanya, dị ka ọ dị na mbụ. Gụọ na mmalite, yabụ mgbanwe chọrọ ịmalitegharịa.                                                                           |
| `CATALOG_BUILD_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `8000` (8s)                            | `src/app/api/v1/models/catalogCache.ts`                                            | echere ụzọ oyi maka mwughachi katalọgụ `GET /v1/models` agbakọtara (#12627). N'oge nkwụsị, a na-enye 200 nke ikpeazụ mgbe otu dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNCED_CATALOG_STALE_AFTER_MS`      | `2592000000` (ụbọchị 30)               | `src/lib/db/models/activeSyncedCatalog.ts`                                         | Afọ ka nke ahụ gasịrị, ndepụta ụdị synced njikọ na-akwụsị ịbụ ikike maka ịkwọgharị wee daa imeghe na ndekọ (#12849). Ahịrị ndị na-enwebeghị akara oge na-agụta dị ka ihe ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60s)                          | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Ogologo oge ole ka a na-ejigharị nzaghachi `GET /api/usage/analytics` nwayọ maka otu eriri ajụjụ ahụ (#15629). `0` gbanyụọ cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_MIN_COMPUTE_MS`     | `1000` (1s)                            | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Naanị nzaghachi nyocha nke were opekata mpe a ogologo a ka echekwara, yabụ obere oge na-ahụ data ọhụrụ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CACHE_METRICS_MIN_COMPUTE_MS`       | unset (gbanyụọ)                        | `src/app/api/cache/route.ts` site na `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`     | Wepụ (#16361). Mgbe atọrọ na integer N na-abụghị nke adịghị mma, akụkụ ojiji-akụkọ ihe mere eme nke `GET /api/cache` (ngwa ngwa-cache ngụkọta + omume) na-eje ozi site na cache nzaghachi nkekọrịta mgbe ọ were opekata mpe N ms iji wuo, kwa windo `trendHours` nke kpọgidere, maka `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS` (akụkọ ihe mere eme nwere ike ịdịruo TTL ahụ; `DELETE /api/cache` na-emebi ya). Akụkọ = ewepụghị ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi na-agụ ozugbo na arịrịọ ọ bụla.                                |
| `NANOBANANA_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `120000`                               | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration.ts`                                             | Max chere maka ọrụ ọgbọ onyonyo NanoBanana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| `CODEBUDDY_SYSTEM_PROMPT_MAX_LEN`              | ∅ (∞) — _unset = enweghị ogologo okpu_ | `open-sse/executors/codebuddy/shared.ts`                                           | Nhọrọ ogologo okpu maka usoro onye na-akpọ oku zitere CodeBuddy (CN na International). Site na ndabara naanị ngwa ngwa na-ebu ihe nrịbama nzuzo-onye nnọchite anya njirimara bụ nke nọpụrụ iche (nzacha ọdịnaya Tencent jụrụ ndị ahụ); A na-edobe mkpali ogologo. Otu integer > 0 na-edochikwa ngwa ngwa sistemụ ọ bụla ogologo oge karịa ọtụtụ mkpụrụedemede; ịtọghe, `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-ewepụ okpu ahụ. Onye ọ bụla nọchiri anya ndekọ nke iwu churu (akara njirimara ma ọ bụ ogologo). |

Olu data ya na-ejigide nzere OAuth/API nke na-eweta ya yana onyonyo etinyere enweghị ụgbọ `api-keys` data dabere na env (naanị `config.yaml` etinyere), yabụ `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere LAN niile ọrụ nwere nzere.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `OPENWA_SERVICE_PORT`                          | `8323`                                 | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                                    | Wepu ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ebe agbakwunyere open-wa (WhatsApp Web automation) daemon na-ege ntị (mgbe niile 127.0.0.1).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` bụ naanị maka relays ndị ọzọ na-anabata ndị ahịa koodu Claude naanị. OmniRoute na-edegharị arịrịọ ka ndị relays nabata ha. Ọ bụrụ naanị na ị chọrọ iji Claude Code CLI, ma ọ bụ na ị maghị ihe relays ndị a, debe nke a nwere nkwarụ ma gbakwunye onye na-eweta Anthropic-dakọtara mgbe niile kama.

## 21. Proxy Health

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                | Ọdabara                  | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Nlele ahụike na-ada ngwa ngwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                     | Window oge (awa) maka ịgbakọ nkezi latency nke proxies ndị chọrọ ntuli aka na atụmatụ ọdọ mmiri emebere nke latency kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Nyocha ahụike cache TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NONE_HONORED_WINDOW_HOURS`                       | `24`                     | `src/lib/db/reasoningEffortCompliance.ts`                                                   | Window na-esochi n'ime awa maka reasoning_effort = ọ nweghị onye na-agụ nnabata na GET /v1/models/health.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NONE_HONORED_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `20000`                  | `src/lib/db/reasoningEffortCompliance.ts`                                                   | Cache TTL na ms maka nseta ihuenyo enweghị nnabata (15-30s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NONE_HONORED_MIN_SAMPLE`                         | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/reasoningEffortCompliance.ts`                                                   | Opekempe, ọ nweghị arịrịọ tupu ihe nlereanya agụ ihe a na-asọpụrụ ma ọ bụ na asọpụrụghị ya; n'okpuru a nlereanya na-agụ amaghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Cache TTL maka nyocha ahụike proxy dara ada. Debe nke a dị mkpụmkpụ karịa `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` ka oge nkwụsị proxy na-adịru nwa oge n'okpuru nnukwu mgbanwe mgbanwe ngwa ngwa na-enweghị gbanyụọ ngwa ngwa-ada maka proxies nwụrụ anwụ n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Tọọ `false` iji gbanyụọ onye nhazi ahụike proxy ndabere nke na-enyocha proxies edebanyere aha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | N'azụ ahụike-onye na-ahazi oge mkpochapụ na ms (nke kacha nta `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`               | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                       | Oge nyocha na ms maka proxies Edge-relay (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), onye nyocha ya na-akpọlite iche iche enweghị ihe nkesa ( `60000` kacha nta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Oge mgbake ndabere na ms: ugboro ole a na-enyocha proxies na-adịghị mma na mbụ ka onye nnọchiteanya eweghachitere laghachi na ntụgharị na-enweghị ịmalitegharị. Uru ndị dị n'okpuru `60000` daa azụ na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip` | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Ebumnuche nyocha ikike iru nke onye nhazi na ebe njedebe `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`. Tụtụ ya aka na internal/self-hosted URL iji zere ndabara ọhaneze.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Nyocha malitere otu ugboro n'otu ogbe, nke onye nhazi oge na ebe njedebe `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-ekekọrịta. N'ala na 1 ma kpuchie na 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Igbu oge na ms n'etiti ọpụpụ nyocha abụọ n'ime ogbe. Na-enweghị ya, batch dum na-ahapụ n'otu oge na egress IP na-ekekọrịta nwere ike imebi ebumnuche nwere oke. Tọọ na `0` iji gbanyụọ oghere; dị na 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                                | Tọọ “ụgha” ka ịkwụsị inyocha ezigbo onye ọbịa nke onye na-eweta proxy ekenyere (`GET /models`, enweghị igodo API ) ma jiri `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` mgbe niile kama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| `EMPTY_STREAM_SIBLING_RECOVERY_ENABLED`           | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-akwado Claude/Codex nyocha ọdịnaya efu na-emefu ihe dị adị na-anwale na njikọ ọzọ tozuru oke mgbe ntụgharị `empty_content` gasịrị, na-enweghị nkwụsịtụ ma ọ bụ mkpuchi ihe nlereanya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_LATE_OUTPUT_PROBE_ENABLED`                | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-aga n'ihu na-enyocha otu nchikota ebugharị na 16 ka 64 nke mbụ a na-ahụ anya ka na-egosi iyi ahụ dị ka mmepụta ewepụtara (mpụta 5xx naanị ya na-arịọ arịrịọ kama mkpuchi naanị ihe nlereanya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` ịmanye, `false`/`0`/`off` ịgbapụ. Mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ (nke ndabara), njikọ igodo API na-arụ ọrụ na-echekwa oke oke na-akpaghị aka. OFF na ndabara (na-ekpuchi njikọ Cooldown / Onye na-eweta Circuit Breaker). Agụnyere nke ukwuu site na ọdịda na-esochi. A na-eji naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

A na-eji naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (echiche) | **Kedu:** mgbake n'ụzọ doro anya nke iyi gbagoro agbago elu (ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri free-claude-code). Jide windo SSE mmeghe ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms) yabụ _pre-commit_ cutoff — nke na-eme tupu byte ọ bụla erute onye ahịa — ga-emepe ọzọ wee nwagharịa na-adịghị ahụ anya.** Mgbe iji mee ka:** flaky/upstreams nke na-emekarị 0-byte-truncate na mmalite iyi; hapụ OFF ma ọ bụrụ na ịnweghị ike ị nweta ihe ruru 750 ms nke akara oge-na-mbụ na iyi ọ bụla. Nabata `true`/`1`/`on`. Mkpụrụ ntọala Resilience nọgidere; ntọala Dashboard na-emeri otu oge atọrọ. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (echiche) | **Kedu:** aga n'ihu n'etiti iyi (Fase 4.4) — mgbe a _post-commit_ truncation (bytes eruola onye ahịa), rịọgharịa ya na akụkụ akụkụ ederede dị ka onye enyemaka prefill wee kpachie suffix efu. Ederede dị larịị OpenAI-dakọtara naanị iyi; Ọ dịghị mgbe ọkụ na ngwá ọrụ oku na-efe efe.** Mgbe iji mee ka:** ogologo ọgbọ na-ebipụ n'etiti azịza na ị na-anabata ọdụ enwetara na-abata dị ka otu gbawara kama token-site-token. N'adabereghị na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (profaịlụ ihe egwu dị iche iche). Nabata `true`/`1`/`on`.                                           |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                | Ịbanye na-arụsi ọrụ ike-iyi bara uru na-ahụ maka mmepụta. Na-achọpụta iyi na-aga n'ihu na-eziga chunks mana ọ nọ n'okpuru ọnụego ntinye aka ahaziri; obi mgbawa, mmemme ojiji, deltas efu, na usoro tool/reasoning adịghị ewere ka ọganihu. Kewapụ na oge arụghị ọrụ na njedebe siri ike. | `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Window mmepụta bara uru na-atụgharị, kechiri ya na 1000–600000 ms; achọrọ otu windo zuru ezu tupu ime ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Ọnụego ntinye ntinye aka UTF-8 kacha nta (proxy token conservative), nwere oke na 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Opekempe arụpụtaghị ihe bara uru na-enweghị efu, a na-ewere na ọ ga-atụ ihe, ejiri ya na 1–1000000 bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Opekempe jitter enweghị usoro (ms) agbakwunyere n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti nlele ahụike onye na-eweta, iji gbochie nwapu (Isiokwu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Oke jitter enweghị usoro (ms) agbakwunyere n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti nlele ahụike onye na-eweta, iji gbochie mgbawa (Isiokwu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                     | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Nleba n'otu oge-nha nha maka mbido mmalite-nyocha ahụike; ụkpụrụ ndị buru ibu na-elele njikọ ndị ọzọ n'otu aka ahụ, ụkpụrụ ndị dị nta na-ebelata ibu mgbawa (Nke #7875, nlọghachi nke #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                      | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                    | Ọnụọgụ nke nnwagharị akpaaka na nzaghachi nju oyi kpara akpa tupu iweghachite mperi na onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                     | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                    | Ogologo oge nkwụghachi azụ kacha (sekọnd) n'etiti nrụgharị oyi. Ejiri uru a kpuchie ya n'agbanyeghị `Retry-After` dị elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`  | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                | Proxy URL. Usoro ndụ dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) na-ebupụta `headroom-ai` CLI mpaghara na loopback na ndabara; mebie naanị iji tụọ aka na proxy sidecar Docker dị na mpụga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Stream-nkwalite nlegharị anya

A na-edozi omume njide mgbake ahụ site na mkpisi aka siri ike na `open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), egosiri ebe a maka ntụnye aka — ịgbanwe ha chọrọ ndezi koodu, ọ bụghị env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — Ogologo oge ole ka echere oghere SSE oghere ka enwere ike ịnwale mkpọpu oge tupu oge ọ bụla etinyere onye ahịa ya.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — okpu siri ike na windo ejidere; eme (flush + passthrough) ozugbo ọtụtụ bytes na-agbakọta, n'agbanyeghị oge.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — max transperent re-mepee nke mgbago iyi ka njide azụ ka na-emebeghị.

> **Oke ọnụ ahịa mmịfe-window onye na-eweta ya (enweghị env var):** FCC-ported per-provinder sliding-window rate-limit _fallback_ dị na koodu (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, wired site `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) mana ụgbọ mmiri nwere** maapụ ndabara efu** ma nwee** enweghị onye ọrụ env var** taa — a na-enyere ya aka naanị site na ntinye nko/dezi koodu. Edepụtaghị ya ụma na tebụl dị n'elu. The per-`(token, IP)` relay limiter nke _does_ nwere mkpịsị aka bụ `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

## 22. Debugging

> [!CAUTION]
> Ụdị mgbanwe ndị a na-emepụta** mpụta ngwaa** ma nwee ike igbapu data nwere mmetụta.** Enwela ike na mmepụta.**

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara                          | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(Edobeghi)_                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tọọ `1` iji mee ka ndekọ mmezu Cursor verbose (nchịkọta SSE emepụtara, wdg).                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | utu aha `CURSOR_DEBUG` dakọtara azụ azụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(Edobeghi)_                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ụzọ faịlụ nhọrọ nke na-enweta chunks Cursor decoded mgbe `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                         | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Gbanye oge nkwụsị na-abaghị uru (ms) maka onye mmebe Cursor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                           | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Window amara (ms) mgbe onye na-ede egwu kv_after_text soft terminator mgbe bytes na-anọgide na-echekwa — na-enye oge oku oku exec_mcp na-esochi iji mechaa etiti ya.                                                                                                                       |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | enyere (`!== "0"`)               | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ngwá ọrụ na-enye ntụziaka nke na-eme composer-2.5 na-enye oku ngwá ọrụ na-atụkwasị obi. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(Edobeghi)_                     | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Sistemu akọwapụtara onye ọrụ ngwa ngwa ederede agbakwunyere na ntụgharị ozi sistemu AFTER (ntụtụ ntụgharị mgbe emechara), rute n'ụzọ codex/Responses na `/v1/messages`. A na-ejikwa ya dị ka nganiihu ntụzịaka wepụrụ na ngọngọ okwu mmalite nke sistemụ. Hapụ na-akpaghị aka iji gbanyụọ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRIP_SYSTEM_PREAMBLE`     | `0` (apụ)                        | `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts`, `open-sse/utils/directivePreambleStripper.ts`     | Tọọ na `1` ka ịwepụ ihe mgbochi ngwa ngwa sistemụ na-amalite site na mmalite iyi openai → claude. Gbanyụọ na ndabara — ndị heuristics na-akpụ akpụ akpụ na-agbanwe ma na-agbanwe ụgwọ nzaghachi, yabụ nzaghachi nke na-emepe nke ọma na ngalaba dị otú ahụ ga-atụfu ya.                    |
| `CURSOR_IMAGE_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `15000`                          | `open-sse/utils/cursorImages.ts`                                                                      | Nweta oge ngwụcha (ms) maka ntinye ọhụụ `image_url` dịpụrụ adịpụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Ewepụghị, onye njikwa na-eji `providerSpecificData.agentBin` wee PATH.                                                                                                                                                          |

Hapụ ndị ọrụ ka ha si na Dashboard kọpụta nsogbu ozugbo.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe    | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                          | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`    | Ebe nchekwa n'ụdị `owner/repo`.                                                                                                          |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`    | GitHub Token nnweta nkeonwe nwere oke `issues:write`.                                                                                    |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(Edobeghi)_ | ndị na-enyere aka n'ụdị triage / igwe ojii | Ihe nrịbama ohere GitHub a na-eji dị ka ọdịda azụ maka `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` ma ndị enyemaka igwe ojii na-eri na `src/lib/cloudAgent/*`. |

## Idobe Ọrụ Ka Ọ Na-arụ na Atụmatụ Render N’efu

Render na-akwụsị ọrụ weebụ n’efu mgbe nkeji 15 gafere na-enweghị okporo ụzọ data na-abata ([oke ọrụ n’efu](https://render.com/docs/free)). Ihe mmalite nọọrọ onwe ya (`scripts/dev/run-standalone.mjs`, nke onyonyo Docker na-eji) nwere ike iziga arịrịọ ugboro ugboro n’oge a kara aka na URL ọha nke ọrụ ahụ. Ọ na-anọgide agbanyụghị ma URL adịghị, na-edekọ mgbe a gbanyere ya, ma emesịa na-akọ naanị mgbanwe na nsonaazụ arịrịọ. Arịrịọ anaghị eso ntụgharị gaa adreesị ọzọ.

| Ihe na-agbanwe               | Uru ndabara                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SELF_PING_ENABLED`          | A na-agbanye mgbe edobere URL | `0`, `false`, `off` ma ọ bụ `no` na-agbanyụ ọrụ iziga ping. Gbanyụọ ya na atụmatụ a na-akwụ ụgwọ ma ọ bụrụ na arịrịọ a na-eziga n’oge a kara aka adịghị mkpa.                                                  |
| `SELF_PING_URL`              | `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL`         | URL ntọala ọha. Render na-enye `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL` maka ọrụ weebụ na saịtị kwụ ọtọ; lee [ntụaka ya maka ihe na-agbanwe na gburugburu](https://render.com/docs/environment-variables).                        |
| `SELF_PING_PATH`             | `/api/monitoring/health`      | Ọ ga-amalite na `/` ma dozie gaa n’otu origin dị ka URL ntọala. URL ma ọ bụ ụzọ na-adịghị irè na-agbanyụ ọrụ iziga ping ma nye ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                 |
| `SELF_PING_INTERVAL_SECONDS` | `180`                         | Sekọnd dị n’etiti arịrịọ; arịrịọ mbụ na-agba mgbe sekọnd 60 gachara. Uru a gụpụtara ga-adị site na `30` ruo `2147483`, gụnyere ókè abụọ ahụ; uru na-adịghị irè ma ọ bụ nọ n’èzí ókè ahụ na-alaghachi na `180`. |

Ọrụ na-agba n’oge niile nke ọnwa nwere ụbọchị 31 na-eji awa instans 744. Tụnyere ojiji ahụ na oke ojiji n’efu nke Render dị ugbu a, nke a na-ekekọrịta n’ofe oghere ọrụ.

## Ọnọdụ Ntinyeọrụ

Maka nduzi SRE nke relay backend (omume ts/bifrost/auto, ebe 9router na CLIProxyAPI kwesịrị ịdị, yana atụmatụ fallback maka nnukwu throughput), lee [Atụmatụ Relay Backend](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mmepe Obodo Dị Mfe

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Mmepụta Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Air-Gapped / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS nwere Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

## 24. Skills Sandbox (v3.8.0 +)

A na-etinye oke na mmado nchekwa mgbe usoro nka (`src/lib/skills/`) na-eme ngwa ngwa akọwapụtara nke onye ọrụ na gburugburu igbe ájá.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                     | Ọdabara                                       | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `10000` (10 s)                                | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oge ngwụcha elekere mgbidi kwa-mgbe maka koodu nka nwere igbe aja. Okpu siri ike; ihe ọ bụla ọzọ na-egbu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`          | _(dabere azụ na `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oge ngwụcha nka nka nka dị elu. Tọọ elu karịa `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` iji kwe ka usoro ọrụ ọtụtụ nzọụkwụ dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`                | `1048576` (1 MB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke bytes nwere ike ịgụ site na faịlụ ọ bụla nwere igbe ájá.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`       | `256000` (250 KB)                             | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke bytes weghaara site na nzaghachi HTTP ọ bụla n'ime nka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS`      | `100000`                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Okpu siri ike na mkpụrụedemede stdout/stderr lọtara site na oku igbe aja.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`       | `false`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tọọ `1`/`true` iji kwe ka netwọk pụọ n'ime igbe ájá. Ihe ndabara **dịpụrụ adịpụ** maka nchekwa. Oge `agent-sandbox` na-eleghara anya: ikewapụ netwọkụ, sistemụ faịlụ na-agụ naanị na oke CPU/memory sitere na `SandboxTemplate` onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`        | _(efu)_                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ndepụta ohere nke rịkọm nke kewapụrụ rịkọm anabatara maka mkpochapụ igbe ájá. Ihe efu pụtara naanị ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`          | _(ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya)_                 | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | A na-eji onyonyo akpa mgbe a na-amịpụta igbe ájá na-akwado Docker. Wepu ka ị tụnye onyonyo ntọala agbasiri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`               | `auto`                                        | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Oge ịgba ọsọ maka igbe igbe nka nka: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman` \| `agent-sandbox`. `auto` na-ahọrọ oge ịgba ọsọ kachasị mma maka onye ọbịa OS (Apple Container/OrbStack na macOS, WSL Container na Windows, Podman na Linux), na-adaghachi azụ na Docker. `agent-sandbox` (ọdọ mmiri ọkụ Kubernetes dịpụrụ adịpụ) bụ Nwepu naanị na achọpụtaghị ya na akpaghị aka. |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_POOLS`           | _(efu)_                                       | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | `image=pool` ụzọ abụọ, rikoma-kewapụrụ: nke `SandboxWarmPool` na-eje ozi ọ bụla oyiyi igbe mgbe `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME=agent-sandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_POOL`            | _(efu)_                                       | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | Ọdọ mmiri na-ekpo ọkụ na-adaghachi azụ maka onyonyo edepụtaghị na `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_POOLS`. A na-agba ọsọ ada (enweghị ọdịda na mpaghara containers) mgbe ọ bụla na-ekpebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_NAMESPACE`       | `default`                                     | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | Oghere aha na-ejide ọdọ mmiri ọkụ; A na-emepụta otu `SandboxClaim` kwa ọsọ na-ehichapụ ebe a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_KUBECONFIG`      | _(kubectl ndabara)_                           | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | Kubeconfig maka oge ịgba ọsọ onye nnọchite-sandbox. Achọrọ naanị igbe igbe create/get/list/watch/delete, pods nweta, pods/exec mepụta na oghere aha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_KUBECTL`         | `kubectl`                                     | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | kubectl ọnụọgụ abụọ nke onye nnọchi anya-sandbox na-eji oge ojiri gaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_CONTAINER`       | `main`                                        | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | Aha arịa dị n'ime igbe ájá nke a na-eme n'ime ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_CLAIM_TIMEOUT_S` | `60`                                          | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | Sekọnd ichere maka nkwupụta ka ọ bụrụ Njikere (max 600). A na-ehichapụ nkwupụta ahụ ma ọ bụrụ na ọ naghị eme ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ịkwado `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` na-emepe ụzọ mgbapụ site na koodu nka aka ike. Jikọta ya na `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` yana amụma `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy siri ike na ntinye aka.

## 25. Oke Ojiji nke Onye Na-enye Ọrụ, Ọwara, Ndabere & Runtime Ndị Ọzọ

Nkwụsị nkwado affinity nke nnọkọ Redis (naanị mgbe ahọpụtara ya; ndabara SQLite agbanwebeghị):

Ụlọ nchekwa affinity Redis ọ bụla na-anabata ihe karịrị arụmọrụ 8 na-echereghị; mgbe ikike juru, oku na-eji SQLite ozugbo, oghere ndị ahụ na-anọgide na-ejide ruo mgbe ọrụ Redis dị n’okpuru kwụsịrị, ọbụna mgbe oge njedebe onye na-akpọ oku nke 100 ms gafere.

- R1. Azịza Redis ọ bụla rutere n’oge maka ọrụ nnọkọ bụ nke a pụrụ ịdabere na ya: mgbanwe ndị a nabatara, CAS ndị a jụrụ ma ọ bụ furu efu, igodo ndị na-adịghị, nhichapụ igodo na-adịghị, na ihe kpatara ma ọ bụ enweghị ihe kpatara eviction na-ekpochapụ ahịrị fallback mpaghara nke nnọkọ ahụ n’otu oge, na-enweghị `await` n’etiti azịza na nhichapụ. Nhichapụ ahụ adịghị emerụ ahụ (n’ọnọdụ kacha njọ, a ga-ahọrọ ọzọ otu ugboro n’oge ọdịda ọzọ); ahịrị ochie adịghị mma.
- R2. Ọrụ nke oge ya agwụla ma ọ bụ nke nwere njehie wee dee fallback mpaghara na-edekọ kpọmkwem ihe o dere dị ka bytes ndị a serialize-rịrị. Ọ bụrụ na iwu Redis ya mechaa n’oge na-abịa, SQLite compare-and-delete nke na-eme n’otu oge na-ekpochapụ naanị ahịrị ahụ nwere bytes ndị ahụ kpọmkwem, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-eji akaụntụ ma ọ bụ njikọ dakọtara. Ihe odide ọdịda ọhụrụ na-adị ndụ.
- R3. Eviction na-ewepụ pin mpaghara nke akaụntụ dara ada mgbe niile, gụnyere n’oge ọdịda; nhazi azịza ya na-esokwa R1/R2. Redis dị mma anaghị ede pins mpaghara: enweghị mirror ma ọ bụ ledger nhazi, ma instances nwere ike ịdị iche n’oge ọdịda.

Endpoint oke ojiji nke onye na-enye ọrụ, ọwara netwọk (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), ọdọ mmiri egress nke 1Proxy, ndabere nchekwa data na obere mgbanwe kwa-feature ndị executor layer ma ọ bụ scripts na-ezo aka na ha.

| Mgbanwe                                        | Ndabara                                                                     | Faịlụ Isi                                                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SESSION_AFFINITY_STORE`             | `sqlite`                                                                    | `src/lib/db/sessionAffinity/storeFactory.ts`                              | Backend maka njikọ session na akaụntụ. Naanị `redis` tinyere `REDIS_URL` ahaziri nke ọma na-eme ka e nwee nkekọrịta; uru a na-edozighị ma ọ bụ nke a na-amaghị, ma ọ bụ nhazi Redis na-efu, na-eji SQLite. Ọ chọrọ `sessionAffinityTtlMs` nke dị mma karịa efu. Nhazi Redis na-echekwa njirimara akaụntụ eji eme ihe nke a kpụchara hash tinyere NJ njikọ; nhazi SQLite ndabara na-edobe ọdịdị JSON ochie nwere mpaghara anọ. Ọ na-adakọ njirimara n'ime ọdọ mmiri mpaghara tozuru etozu tupu NJ. Ọ na-eji `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX` nke onye ahịa nkekọrịta, ụkpụrụ JSON na `PX` TTL; mgbanwe na-atụnyere uru a hụrụ kpọmkwem. Azịza Redis dị mma na-ewepụ fallback mpaghara nke nkwụsị ọrụ, azịza bịara n'oge na-adịghị anya nwekwara ike ihichapụ naanị ahịrị fallback serialized nke kpọmkwem ọrụ ahụ gafere oge dere. A na-enyocha pins e bubatara tupu eji ha ọzọ. Njehie Redis, oge gafere ma ọ bụ saturation nnabata na-alaghachi na SQLite site na ịdọ aka ná ntị ndị a na-amachi ugboro ha (oke 100 ms kwa oku store); Redis misses gara nke ọma anaghị agụ shadow. Ọ naghị ekekọrịta combo stickiness, native Codex turn pins, concurrency akaụntụ, lockouts ma ọ bụ oke mmefu. |
