## 26. Ngwá Nnwale & E2E

Nke a bụ ihe `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, na `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs` na-eji. Hapụ ihe ọ bụla dị n'okpuru ka ọ bụrụ nke a na-ejibeghị na ndepụta mmepụta (production).

| Variable                               | Nke dabara adaba                    | Faịlụ isi                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| -------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ọnọdụ bootstrap nke E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) maka onye na-agba ọsọ Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | na-akwụghachi na `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Okwuntughe nchịkwa etinyere na gburugburu Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanye ihe nrụlele ahụike obodobere na-agba ọsọ n'oge Playwright na-agba ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanye usoro ihe nrụlele ahụike token OAuth n'oge nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(a na-ejibeghị ya)_                | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Ndị na-enye ọrụ a kpọsara site na koma nke a na-ewepụ na nlele mmelite token a na-eme tupu ọ bụla (dịka `codex,openai`). Nhọrọ a họọrọ karia ịgbanye ihe nrụlele ahụike dum — ndị na-enye ọrụ nwere TTL dị mkpirikpi na-aga n'ihu na-emelite ọhụrụ ebe ndị na-enye ọrụ cascade ka na-anọ na-nhọrọ na-aza ọzụzụ naanị. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mechie mkpọtụ ihe nrụlele ahụike na stdout nke Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Wụlie mmepụta mmepụta Next.js tupu Playwright amalite (nkwalite CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                                 | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Wụlie hook iwepụ nke OmniRoute (nke CI na-eji iji nwee `node_modules` ka ọ dị mma).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                            | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Oge echi (ms) maka sava ịdị ahụike tupu a malite ịgba nnwale usoro ihe / ụkpụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`      | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL nke ngwá smoke nke Electron na-atụ anya na ngwa a na-ebu na-enye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Oge ime kaadị dum (ms) tupu ngwá smoke a kwụsị ịnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ọzụzụ ime kaadị (ms) ka ibe a na-ebu gburugburu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(na-akpali onwe ya)_               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ụzọ doro anya gaa na executable nke Electron a na-ebu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                          | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Nchịkọta data maka ọsọ smoke nke Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Debe `1` iji debe ntuziaka data smoke ahụ mgbe ọgba ahụ gasịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Debe `1` iji bufee ndekọ Electron na stdout n'oge ọgba ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: malitegharịa megide otu dir data ahụ wee kwenye na mwepụta nke abụọ họrọrọ onye ọkwọ SQLite obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(nchọpụta PATH)_                   | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Gafere ụzọ binary Devin CLI ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

### Pipeline ntụgharị asụsụ Docs

`scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` na-eji ya (iwu `npm run i18n:run` ahụ).
A naghị edobe variables isii ahụ niile n'ụzọ izizi — debe ha na `.env` naanị na ngwá
ndị kwesịrị ịgba onye ntụgharị docs ahụ.

| Variable                                   | Izizi                   | Faịlụ isi mmalite                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`            | _(a naghị edobe)_       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | URL isi kwadoro OpenAI maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`            | _(a naghị edobe)_       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Token Bearer maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ ahụ (anaghị edekọ ya).                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`              | _(a naghị edobe)_       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | ID ụdị, dịka `gpt-4o-mini` ma ọ bụ `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`    | _(a naghị edobe)_       | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Usoro ụdị nhọrọ kewapụrụ site na comma nke a na-anwale n'usoro mgbe ụdị bụ isi dara oda (429 quota, 401/402, 5xx, oge agwụla, netwọk); anaghị anwale arịrị aghụghọ (400/413/422) ọzọ.                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | `60000`                 | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Oge agwụla arịrị ọ bụla na milliseconds.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT`   | _(a naghị edobe)_       | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Njikwa reasoning nhọrọ, nke a na-eziga azụ dị ka ọ dị mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`        | `4` (LLM), `8` (Google) | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ọrụ ntụgharị faịlụ/asụsụ mpaghara ndị na-aga n'otu oge. `--concurrency` na-akagbu ntọala gburugburu ebe ahụ; a na-achịkwa ọnụọgụ arịrịọ Google na-aga n'otu oge iche n'okpuru.                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_ENGINE`             | `llm`                   | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | `llm` na-eji backend LLM ahaziri. `google` na-ahọrọ Google dị ka injin bụ isi ma ọ chọghị mgbanwe backend LLM.                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_FALLBACK`    | `1`                     | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | A na-agbanye ya na ndabara mgbe a na-eji injin LLM: mgbe ntụgharị ibe ọdịnaya dara ka e mechara nnwale nkwado ọzọ ma ọ bụ njehie ụdị, a na-anwale Google dị ka nhọrọ nkwado ma na-enyocha nsonaazụ ya. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_API_KEY`     | _(edoghị)_              | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Igodo Cloud Translation v2 nke a na-ahọrọ iji. Mgbe edoghị ya, arịrịọ na-eji endpoint weebụ na-enweghị igodo.                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_CONCURRENCY` | `6`                     | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Ọnụọgụ zuru oke karịrị efu nke na-amachi arịrịọ Google ndị na-aga n'otu oge, nke asụsụ mpaghara ndị na-eji otu oke ahaziri n'ime otu usoro na-ekekọrịta. A na-ajụ ụkpụrụ na-ezighi ezi tupu arịrịọ ọ bụla.                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`                 | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Oge nkwụsị maka arịrịọ ọ bụla n'ime milisekọnd, dị ka ọnụọgụ zuru oke sitere na `1` ruo `2147483647`. A na-ajụ ụkpụrụ na-ezighi ezi tupu arịrịọ ọ bụla.                                                                        |
| `UI_KIT_READ_TOKEN`                        | _(Edobeghi)_            | `scripts/check/check-ui-kit-upstream.mjs` | Token naanị ịgụ maka elu UI kit repo nke onwe, nke `npm run check:ui-kit-upstream` na-eji yana usoro ọrụ "UI Kit upstream watch" kwa ụbọchị. Na mpaghara nlele ahụ na-eji nzere git gị.                                        |

## 27. Nri radar ( Bochum nkeonwe)

Ihe mgbakwunye nhọrọ nke ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED gbara (ndapụtara — ọkọlọtọ njirimara na-atụgharị site na Settings/DB, ọ bụghị env var; lee [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off) ). Ngbanwe anọ ndị mbụ dị n'okpuru bụ nhọrọ nhọrọ maka ntinye aka nke onwe ma ọ bụ ndụdụ na isi igodo nkwado. Nke ise, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, bụ njikọ dị iche na-enweghị ndabara na panel arụmọrụ nke onye nwe ya. Hụ [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) maka doc modul zuru oke yana [mmalite ya na ngwụcha ngwụcha yana usoro nhazi eduzi](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Onye na-agụ ọkwa ọkwa Home/Changelog anaghị ahazi ya site na mgbanwe gburugburu ebe obibi na ọ dabereghị na ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED. Ọ na-agụ ebe nchekwa ọha `news.json` URL ekwuputara na `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` na GET naanị; IDs nchụpụ ka dị na nchekwa ihe nchọgharị mpaghara.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe            | Ọdabara                                      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | Base URL kesara site na katalọgụ bịanyere aka na ya iche, ntinye aka, onyinye nkwado na nri Intel. Wepụtụ aka na ọrụ ejiri aka gị ma ọ bụ nke agbadoro anya. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(igodo ndabara etinyere)_                   | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Ed25519 igodo ọha (base64-DER SPKI ma ọ bụ PEM) ejiri iji nyochaa mbinye aka nri site na ndepụta ahaziri iche.                                               |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtịnụ dashboard "Abụ m onye ntinye aka ga-emepe (GitHub OAuth supporter-key claim flow).                                                                |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtịnụ dashboard "kwado ọrụ ahụ" ga-emepe ( ibe payment/plans ).                                                                                         |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(Edobeghi)_                                 | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Njikọ ọrụ naanị onye nwe ya-njikọ. achọrọ HTTPS belụsọ maka HTTP loopback SSH n'ihu; ụkpụrụ atọpụghị ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi emepụtaghị ihe nsoroụzọ.             |

---

## Nyocha: Wepụrụ / Nwụrụ Anwụ mgbanwe

Ụdị mgbanwe ndị a pụtara na nsụgharị `.env.example` gara aga mana nwere** enweghị ntụaka oge ojiri gaa** na codebase dị ugbu a. E wepụrụ ha:

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                                                                                                                                              | Ihe kpatara ya                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Agaghị agụ site na faịlụ isi mmalite ọ bụla. SQLite bụ naanị ọkwọ ụgbọala na-akwado — enweghị nhọrọ achọrọ.                                |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Gaa na ndebiri docs/env ochie mana ejighi ya n'oge ojiri gaa. Nwere ike ịlaghachi na njirimara ọtụtụ ihe atụ n'ọdịnihu.                    |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Edeghị ya na koodu isi mmalite. Ọnweghị oke nchekwa data nwere oke n'ụzọ artificial.                                                       |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Edeghị ya na koodu isi mmalite. O yikarịrị ka ewepụrụ nhichapụ ihe nketa.                                                                  |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Edebanyeghị aha na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                    |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Edebanyeghị aha na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` (Kimi Coding na-eji OAuth, ọ bụghị ọnụọgụ abụọ CLI ).                               |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Edeghị ebe ọ bụla na koodu isi mmalite.                                                                                                    |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | Ewepụrụ na v3.8.0. Oge ịgba ọsọ anaghịzi agụ nzere env vars — ndị a sitere na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / ezoro ezo DB. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | Ewepụrụ na v3.8.0. Cursor executor na-eji `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (lee §22).                                                |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ na v3.8.0. Kiro nọ na `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — ngbanwe ya enweghị mmetụta.                                              |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na ndị ọzọ na-eweta API isi mkpịsị ugodi na v3.8.0.                                                                             |

### Mmezi Uru Ọsọ

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe        | Uru `.env.example` ochie | Ndebanye aha koodu | Edoziri                                                     |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                     | `7`                | ✅ wepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; edepụtara `7` dị ka ndabere |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                     | `7`                | ✅ wepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; edepụtara `7` dị ka ndabere |

### OpenCode config regeneration (ad-hoc tooling)

Nke `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` na-eji weghachi `opencode.json` na ụkpụrụ `limit.context` na `limit.output` ziri ezi ewepụtara na OmniRoute na-agba ọsọ. Ọ dịghị nke ọ bụla n'ime ihe ndị a achọrọ maka ịrụ ọrụ nkịtị — edemede ahụ bụ naanị onye nrụpụta.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Ọdabara                  | Isi mmalite faịlụ                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Ntọala URL nke ihe atụ OmniRoute iji jụọ ajụjụ maka `/v1/models`.                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(Edobeghi)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | igodo API iji nyochaa megide ebe njedebe `/v1/models` OmniRoute. Daghachi azụ na `OPENCODE_API_KEY` mgbe ịtọghị ya.          |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(Edobeghi)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API ụdị OpenCode (`sk-...`) edere n'ime `opencode.json` eweghachitere. Daghachi azụ na `OMNIROUTE_KEY` mgbe ịtọghị ya. |

### Mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị-eval njigide (ad-hoc tooling)

`scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` ji ya , nyocha mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị CLI. Achọghị maka ọrụ nkịtị — onye nrụpụta ngwaọrụ naanị.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe           | Ọdabara    | Isi mmalite faịlụ                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (efu) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Asambodo JSON nke onye ọrụ wetara maka onye na-eweta nke mkpakọ-eval CLI na-anọghị n'ịntanetị gosipụtara (jiri `JSON.parse` tụgharịrị). Hapụ na-edozighị maka ọsọ akọrọ. |

### VNC Nchọgharị

Nke `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` na-eji hazie oge Chromium enweghị isi nke dabere na Docker maka ndị na-eweta ihe nchọgharị-akpaaka. Ihe ndabara — niile ahọpụtara egosiri n'okpuru.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara                        | Isi mmalite faịlụ                           | Nkọwa                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ na ọnụọgụ abụọ Docker a na-eji amịpụta arịa VNC.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | foto Docker maka akpa VNC Chromium.                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(ọkọlọtọ arụnyere n'ime ya)_  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Extra Chromium CLI args gafere na ihe nchọgharị dị n'ime akpa ahụ.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | VNC ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri n'ime akpa.                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Chrome DevTools Protocol ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri n'ime akpa.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha n'ime akpa ahụ.                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Akwụkwọ ndekọ nke ndị ọbịa maka profaịlụ ihe nchọgharị na-adịgide adịgide.      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) na-adịghị arụ ọrụ tupu e weta nnọkọ VNC.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ogologo oge kachasị (ms).                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge VNC kacha n'otu oge.                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ngwụcha njikere ihe nchọgharị (ms).                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Harvest/cleanup nkwụsị (ms).                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọk Docker raara onwe ya nye, akpa ahụ na-esonye (#12571), pụọ na akwa akwa. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha maka Notion eri oge nnọgidesi ike.                            |

### Ntinye ọrụ ime

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                      | Ọdabara | Nkọwa                                                                                |
| --------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –       | Inline token maka njikwa-ụgbọelu ọrụ-na-ọrụ nyocha.                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –       | Uzo gaa na faịlụ nwere akara ọrụ ime (ọ masịrị ya na arịa; na-ewepụ mgbanwe inline). |

### onye na-eweta OpenRouter stats

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                  | Ọdabara   | Nkọwa                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Tọọ `false` ka ịwụpụ ịweta OpenRouter stats onye na-eweta maka nkwalite katalọgụ. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | Cache TTL (ms) maka stats ndị na-eweta OpenRouter ewetara.                        |

### agbakwunyere Redis njide

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe          | Ọdabara     | Nkọwa                                                                 |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Kechie adreesị maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                           |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Port maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | OmniRoute kpuchiri ekpuchi maka adreesị ejikọtara Redis agbakwunyere. |

## 24. Ihe agbakwunyere na mbipụta v3.8.50

E webatara ntọala ndị a mgbe e sere foto ọnọdụ nkwekọrịta gburugburu ebe obibi gara aga.

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                                 | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ogologo oge kachasị a ga-echere oghere nnabata nkata na-eri nnukwu akụrụngwa tupu eweghachi `503` enwere ike ịnwalegharị; obere nchere nwere oke na-ahazi mwubata ndị nnọchiteanya n’usoro kama iweghachi `503` ozugbo. `0` na-eweghachi ọjụjụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Oke byte ndị nọ n’ahịrị maka nchere nnabata: ọ na-amachi mkpokọta byte nke ahụ arịrịọ echekwara nwa oge n’ofe usoro ahụ dum ka nchere ahụ ghara ịbawanye heap (#4380). Nchere ndị gafere oke ahụ na-enweta `503` enwere ike ịnwalegharị ozugbo.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60 s)                         | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ọ bụ no-op a kwụsịrị ịkwado kemgbe #10110: e wepụrụ ụzọ nnabata nke nnọkọ ọ bụla iji jiri otu oke metụtara usoro ahụ dum dochie ha. A na-anabata ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                                   | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ọ bụ no-op a kwụsịrị ịkwado kemgbe #10110: e wepụrụ ụzọ nnabata nke nnọkọ ọ bụla iji jiri otu oke metụtara usoro ahụ dum dochie ha. A na-anabata ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_LANES`              | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                        | `open-sse/services/admission/runtime.ts`                                                 | Ụzọ nnabata mebere nke runtime na-emegharị onwe ha (#9654): isi mgba ọkụ maka ọnụ ụzọ na-emegharị onwe ya nke onye nwe ọrụ ọ bụla (sistemụ 2). Ọ dị iche na mgbanwe ụzọ nke njikọ ọ bụla a kwụsịrị ịkwado n’elu (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, no-op kemgbe #10110). Ọ bụ ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ dashboard nwere otu aha ahụ; env var na-aka ngbanwe dashboard ike; ọ chọrọ ịmalitegharị.        |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Na-amachi oge oku run-now ga-echere ọrụ na-aga n’ihu tupu ọ malite ọrụ ahụ dị n’ahịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | agbanyere                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-eme ka ọnọdụ IMS na ihe ize ndụ ihe nchọgharị dị ọhụrụ site na nnọkọ Chrome CDP dabere na akaụntụ; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | agbanyere                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-echekwa nnọkọ Adobe ndị arụziri n’okpuru `DATA_DIR`; tọọ `0` maka ọnọdụ dị naanị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`           | `12000`                                | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oghere oge kacha nta n’etiti nnyefe ọ bụla maka imepụta ihe na Adobe Firefly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                                | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oge mgbakwunye a na-emeghị ihe mgbe nnyefe Adobe nke atọ ọ bụla gara nke ọma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`             | `0`                                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`                                          | Ọnọdụ true-headless maka nchọpụta njehie naanị; Adobe colligo na-ajụkarị nnọkọ ihe ize ndụ nke sitere na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHROME_PATH`                               | nchọpụta-akpaghị aka                   | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`, `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts` | Faịlụ Chrome executable zuru oke a na-ahọrọ, nke ndị executor na-achịkwa ihe nchọgharị na-eji mgbe nchọpụta-akpaghị aka nke ikpo okwu ezughị oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`                        | _(unset)_                              | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Token BotFather nke na-eme ka webhook mbata rụọ ọrụ ma na-eji mbinye aka echekwa `initData` nke Mini App.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET`                   | _(unset)_                              | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Ihe nzuzo nkekọrịta e debanyere aha site na `setWebhook` ma na-enyocha ya megide header `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` na nnyefe webhook ọ bụla. Ọ dị mkpa maka ụzọ webhook; ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, a ga-eji 503 jụ nnyefe webhook.                                                                                                                                               |
| `TELEGRAM_DEFAULT_MODEL`                    | `auto/chat`                            | `src/lib/telegram/chatProxy.ts`                                                          | Model a na-eji maka nzaghachi nkata Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_API_BASE`                     | `https://api.telegram.org`             | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Nhọrọ URL ntọala Bot API ọzọ maka proxy ma ọ bụ sava Bot API ndị a na-akwado n'onwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Oge nkwụsị n'ime milisekọnd maka oku Bot API na-apụ apụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `TELEGRAM_USER_ALLOWLIST_ENABLED`           | `false`                                | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Tọọ ya na `true` ka a na-ejere naanị ndị ọrụ Telegram e depụtara na `TELEGRAM_ALLOWED_USER_IDS` ozi (ndị ọzọ: Mini App na-eweghachi 403, a na-atụfu ozi webhook). A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara: a na-ejere onye ọrụ Telegram ọ bụla ozi, a na-emepụtakwara ya igodo API nke na-eji oke ojiji onye na-eweta ọrụ upstream.                                                               |
| `TELEGRAM_ALLOWED_USER_IDS`                 | _(unset)_                              | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | ID nọmba nke ndị ọrụ Telegram, ndị rịkọm kewapụrụ, a na-ejere ozi mgbe `TELEGRAM_USER_ALLOWLIST_ENABLED=true`. A na-eleghara ntinye ndị na-abụghị integer karịrị efu e dere n'ụdị nkịtị anya, na-enye ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ugboro. A na-eleghara ntọala a anya mgbe agbanyụrụ ndepụta ndị e kwere; ọ bụrụ na agbanyere ndepụta ahụ ma ọ tọgbọ chakoo, a naghị ejere onye ọ bụla ozi. |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPTIONAL_PACK_TAR`               | `1` (agbanyere)                        | `scripts/build/optionalPackStaging.mjs`                                                  | Tọọ `0` ka a ghara iwepụta tarball `.tar.gz` mgbe a na-akwadebe ngwugwu ML/ihe nchọgharị nhọrọ maka osisi Electron standalone (a ka ga-emepụta ndekọ ngwugwu na `optional-packs.index.json`). Usoro ọrụ mwepụta desktọpụ na-eji nke a belata nha artifact a na-ebugote.                                                                                                            |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED`          | `false`                                | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`                                                       | Na-eme ka ndebanye aha ụzọ onyonyo ndenye aha xAI rụọ ọrụ; agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara ka ụzọ onyonyo API-key ghara ịgbanwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`             | `60000`                                | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                                                     | Oge izu ike nke key ọ bụla n'ime milisekọnd mgbe nzaghachi `429` enweghị ntụziaka `Retry-After` a pụrụ iji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`           | agbanyere                              | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts`                                              | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off`, `0`, ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ ndekọ na itinye calibration estimator dị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_MANUAL_SIG_HANDLE`                    | _(internal)_                           | `scripts/dev/standalone-server-ws.mjs`                                                   | Nhọrọ ime nke na-anọchi signal-handler nke standalone-server; launcher na-edokarị ma na-ehichapụ ya kama ndị operator ịhazi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                               | `open-sse/utils/chatgptWebExecutorAdapter.ts`                                            | Oke oge kwa turn ederede nke ChatGPT Web (Clean Room); a na-eleghara uru dị n'okpuru `1000` anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_TOOL_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `180000`                               | `open-sse/utils/chatgptWebExecutorAdapter.ts`                                            | Oke oge kwa turn ChatGPT Web (Clean Room) nke nwere ngwaọrụ client (envelope `<tool>` e mere ka ọ dị ka nke mbụ); belata ya iji weghachi njikwa na client ngwa ngwa. A na-eleghara uru dị n'okpuru `1000` anya.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_HEADLESS`                      | `1` (na-enweghị ihu eserese)           | `open-sse/utils/chatgptWebExecutorAdapter.ts`                                            | Na-eme ka browser ChatGPT Web (Clean Room) rụọ ọrụ na-enweghị ihu eserese. Tinye `0` naanị maka akaụntụ nke ule ọrụ ahụ n'onwe ya jụrụ ọnọdụ enweghị ihu eserese (ndabara gbanwere site na nke nwere ihu gaa na nke na-enweghị ihu na #14948).                                                                                                                                     |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_ACQUIRE_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `45000`                                | `open-sse/utils/chatgptWebExecutorAdapter.ts`                                            | Oke oge maka ịmalite browser ChatGPT Web (Clean Room) na ịkwadebe chatgpt.com, ka browser ma ọ bụ session na-adịghị arụ ọrụ ghara iri oge niile nke kwụ n'ahịrị arịrịọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `UI_KIT_CLONE`                              | `_references/cheaper-inference-ui-kit` | `scripts/check/check-ui-kit-snapshot.mjs`                                                | Ụzọ nke klọn UI Kit CI nkeonwe nke ọnụ ụzọ vendored-snapshot ji atụnyere `packages/ui-kit/src` (KEA-7886); ọnụ ụzọ ahụ na-amafe ya ma nye ịdọ aka ná ntị mgbe klọn ahụ adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Ntọala ndabara zuru ụwa ọnụ maka gburugburu oge nrụọrụ ihe nchọgharị na ọwara ngwaọrụ na-apụ apụ. Uru njikọ ahaziri na dashboard na-ebute ụzọ.

| Ngbanwe                                 | Ndabara                        | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(nchọpụta-akpaghị aka)_       | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Ụzọ Chrome/Chromium akọwapụtara maka nrụnye npm, systemd na PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(nchọpụta-akpaghị aka)_       | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Nhọrọ ndabere nkịtị maka ụzọ Chrome/Chromium akọwapụtara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Ebe njedebe CDP dị n'ime; Docker na-eji sidecar dị na ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(edobeghị — agbanyụrụ)_       | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`                   | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` (ma hazie ebe njedebe CDP) iji jiri profaịlụ ihe nchọgharị a na-ejikwa site n'èzí, nke abanyelarị, na ibe ChatGPT ya dị ugbu a kama iji context dịpụrụ iche n'oge ntụgharị ọ bụla; ọ na-amachikwa taabụ ChatGPT ndị nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge ka ha bụrụ 1. A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara. Hụ [profaịlụ CDP na-adịgide adịgide](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md). |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                  | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                                | Nzuzo a na-ekekọrịta nke a chọrọ n'ime nkụnyeisi `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` maka arịrịọ HTTP ọ bụla nke proxy CDP na nkwalite WebSocket. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, proxy ahụ na-ebunye ma ọ bụ na-emepụta token site n'iji `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN_FILE`; ma ọ bụrụ na enweghị token a pụrụ iji, ọ ga-ajụ arịrịọ niile. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                  |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | ID tunnel OpenAI zuru ụwa ọnụ maka ntụgharị ngwá ọrụ Codex mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Igodo runtime tunnel zuru ụwa ọnụ; etinyekwala ya mgbe ọ bụla na ndekọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Aha kpọmkwem nke connector omenala ChatGPT e mepụtara ọhụrụ maka njikọ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Ndekọ pụrụ iche maka ọnọdụ ihe nchọgharị, broker na tunnel.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-diagnostics.ts` | Mgbe edobere ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`, ọ na-echekwa onyonyo nchọpụta nsogbu ihe nchọgharị n'ebe nlele ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na faịlụ binary launcher na-adịgide adịgide.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(nchọpụta-akpaghị aka)_       | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na faịlụ binary runtime Bun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Nhọrọ ndabere ochie maka `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; nrụnye ọhụrụ na-eji aha ọkọlọtọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

---

## OmniConductor Bridge

Ndị ahịa SSE dị ogologo ndụ nke na-egosipụta ọrụ OmniConductor hub n'ime A2A TaskManager (`src/lib/conductor/`). Ịbanye — akwa na-amalite naanị mgbe edobere `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Naanị akụkụ nkesa: akara hub agaghị erute ihe nchọgharị ahụ.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe             | Ọdabara                 | Isi mmalite faịlụ                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Ndabere URL nke ọdụ OmniConductor (dịka `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Akwụsịla akwa mmiri =.                              |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | nzere Hub maka nri — SSE na-ebunye ndị ọgbọ `spokesperson` -kind na hub (`POST /v1/peers`, admin).                    |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | nzere maka mbata A2A→ hub ọrụ ndị nnọchi anya (`POST /v1/tasks`); daa azụ na `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` mgbe ịtọgheghị ya. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | Base URL nke ọrụ ọnụ na-ekwuchitere (Faro) n'azụ dashboard nkata proxy (`/api/conductor/ask`).                        |

### Nhazi nhazi ọkwa-mmata

Ndị `open-sse/services/combo.ts` na `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` na-eji maka nlele ego mmefu ego tupu arịrịọ. Ịbanye — omume mbugharị ndabara adịghị agbanwe agbanwe mgbe ewepụtaghị ya.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                 | Ọdabara | Isi mmalite faịlụ                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`     | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Mgbe `1`, kwue njikọ nke mmefu ego nrịbama nke otu windo (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, table `provider_quota_state`) enweghị ike ị nweta ọnụ ahịa arịrịọ echere tupu izipu ya. Ọdịda-mepee mgbe ahazighị mmefu ego.                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(na)_  | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Nhazi nzaghachi nke ihe nleba anya ihe nleba anya gburugburu-nche (#14931): kwa (onye na-eweta, ihe nlereanya, ngwaọrụ dị ugbu a) EMA nke `usage.prompt_tokens` / atụmatụ, agbakwunyere na 0.15–2.0, na ebe nchekwa naanị. `off`, `0` ma ọ bụ `false` gbanyụọ ndekọ na ngwa. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false` | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Na-edeba aha `xai-oauth` (`xao`) na `grok-cli` ụzọ onyonyo. Dashboard atụmatụ ọkọlọtọ nke otu aha; mwepu DB meriri uru a.                                                                                                                                                    |

## Nchọpụta mmekọrịta nke ụdị

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe             | Ọdabara | Isi mmalite faịlụ                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------ | ------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MODEL_SYNC_TIMINGS` | `0`     | `src/lib/providerModels/modelSyncTimings.ts` | Tọọ `1` ka ịdebanye oge oge, arịrịọ ID ewepụtara na nsonaazụ success/error nke oge n'oge mmekọrịta ụdị. Agbanyụrụ maka ụkpụrụ ndị ọzọ niile. Ndekọ na-ahapụ arịrịọ, akaụntụ, katalọgụ na ibu akwụ ụgwọ mperi. |

## Ngwa OpenCode nyocha mgbasa ozi nke mpaghara

` scripts/cli/omniroute-media-mcp.mjs` na-eji ya. Tọọ ndị a na gburugburu usoro ngwa mpaghara.

| Mgbanwe                    | Ndabara                                                                                | Nkọwa                                                                                     |
| -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_CONFIG`   | `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/opencode/opencode.json` (ma ọ bụ `~/.config/opencode/opencode.json`) | Nhazi OpenCode nke ngwa mgbasa ozi na onye nrụnye na-agụ.                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_PROVIDER` | `omniroute`                                                                            | Ntinye onye na-enye ọrụ nke ngwa mgbasa ozi na-agụ site na nhazi OpenCode.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_MODEL`    | `auto`                                                                                 | Model nke ngwa mgbasa ozi na-enyefe. Onye na-enye ọrụ ya ga-akwado ụdị mgbasa ozi achọrọ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_API_KEY`  | Igodo API onye na-enye ọrụ ahaziri ma ọ bụ ntụaka gburugburu ya                        | Ndochi nzere nhọrọ. Debe ya na gburugburu usoro ngwa mpaghara; etinyela ya na commit.     |

## Nlekota nnọkọ

| Mgbanwe                     | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi                             | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SESSION_TTL_MIN` | `15`    | `open-sse/services/sessionManager.ts` | TTL nnọkọ na-anaghị arụ ọrụ, na nkeji, maka iji njikọ e kenyere ọzọ na ile nnọkọ ndị na-arụ ọrụ. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite; njehie ntụgharị na ụkpụrụ `<= 0` na-eji `15`. |
